?= emacs\nEMACSCLIENT ?= emacsclient\n\n.DEFAULT_GOAL := check\n\n# All project Elisp files, excluding Git internals.\nEL_FILES := $(sort $(shell find . -type d -name .git -prune -o \\\n\t-type f -name '*.el' -print))\nEL_DIRS := $(sort $(dir $(EL_FILES)))\nTEST_FILES := $(filter %-test.el %-tests.el,$(EL_FILES))\n\nEMACS_BATCH = $(EMACS) -Q --batch \\\n\t$(foreach dir,$(EL_DIRS),-L \"$(dir)\") \\\n\t--eval '(setq load-prefer-newer t)'\n\n\n# These expressions must not contain shell single quotes.\ndefine CHECK_SYNTAX\n(let ((files command-line-args-left))\n  (setq command-line-args-left nil)\n  (dolist (file files)\n    (message \"Checking %s\" file)\n    (with-temp-buffer\n      (insert-file-contents file)\n      (emacs-lisp-mode)\n      (check-parens))))\nendef\n\n# Use absolute paths: the server may have a different working directory.\ndefine INDENT_FILES\n(let ((files (quote ($(foreach file,$(EL_FILES),\"$(abspath $(file))\")))))\n  (save-current-buffer\n    (dolist (file files)\n      (message \"Indenting %s\" file)\n      (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect file)\n        (save-excursion\n          (save-restriction\n            (widen)\n            (indent-region (point-min) (point-max))))\n        (save-buffer))))\n  (format \"Indented %d files\" (length files)))\nendef\n\n.PHONY: check lint syntax compile test format clean\n\n# Explicit sequencing also keeps \"make -j check\" safe.\ncheck:\n\t$(MAKE) clean\n\t$(MAKE) lint\n\t$(MAKE) test\n\nclean:\n\t$(RM) $(EL_FILES:.el=.elc)\n\ncompile:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) \\\n\t\t--eval '(setq byte-compile-error-on-warn t)' \\\n\t\t-f batch-byte-compile $(EL_FILES)\nformat:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) --eval '$(strip $(INDENT_FILES))'\n\nlint:\n\t$(MAKE) syntax\n\t$(MAKE) compile\n\nsyntax:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) --eval '$(strip $(CHECK_SYNTAX))' $(EL_FILES)\n\ntest:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) \\\n\t\t$(foreach file,$(TEST_FILES),-l $(file)) \\\n\t\t-f ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit\n","truncated":null}}

ende-test.el --- ERT tests for ende -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter\n;; Author: Peter\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools, llm\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; ERT coverage for synchronous operations, asynchronous lifecycle behavior,\n;; target conflicts, and the incremental streaming parsers.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'ert)\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'json)\n(require 'ende-provider)\n(require 'ende)\n\n;;;; Test harness\n\n(defvar ende-test--prompts nil)\n(defvar ende-test--calls nil)\n(defvar ende-test--fake-responses nil)\n\n(defun ende-test--callback (call name &rest args)\n  \"Invoke callback NAME from CALL with ARGS when it exists.\"\n  (let ((callback (plist-get (plist-get call :callbacks) name)))\n    (when callback\n      (apply callback args))))\n\n(defun ende-test--overlay-label (overlay)\n  \"Return the display label attached to OVERLAY.\"\n  (substring-no-properties\n   (or (overlay-get overlay 'before-string)\n       (overlay-get overlay 'after-string)\n       \"\")))\n\n(defun ende-test--complete-call (call)\n  \"Complete asynchronous fake CALL using its queued response.\"\n  (let ((response (plist-get call :response)))\n    (cond\n     ((eq response :stall) nil)\n     ((eq response :error)\n      (ende-test--callback\n       call :on-error\n       (ende-error-create :kind 'mock :message \"mock failure\")))\n     ((stringp response)\n      (ende-test--callback call :on-event\n                           (list :type :status :status \"Generating\"))\n      (ende-test--callback call :on-event\n                           (list :type :delta :channel :content\n                                 :text response))\n      (ende-test--callback call :on-event\n                           (list :type :usage :tokens 10 :speed 50.0))\n      (ende-test--callback call :on-done response))\n     ((and (listp response) response)\n      (when (or (plist-get response :reported-model)\n                (plist-get response :reported-provider))\n        (ende-test--callback\n         call :on-event\n         (list :type :metadata\n               :metadata\n               (list :source :response\n                     :model (plist-get response :reported-model)\n                     :provider (plist-get response :reported-provider)))))\n      (when (stringp (plist-get response :thinking))\n        (ende-test--callback\n         call :on-event\n         (list :type :delta :channel :thinking\n               :text (plist-get response :thinking))))\n      (when (stringp (plist-get response :content))\n        (ende-test--callback\n         call :on-event\n         (list :type :delta :channel :content\n               :text (plist-get response :content))))\n      (when (plist-member response :usage)\n        (ende-test--callback\n         call :on-event\n         (list :type :usage\n               :tokens (plist-get response :tokens)\n               :speed (plist-get response :speed)\n               :usage (plist-get response :usage))))\n      (when (listp (plist-get response :partial))\n        (ende-test--callback\n         call :on-event\n         (list :type :partial-result :result (plist-get response :partial))))\n      (ende-test--callback call :on-done response))\n     (t\n      (ende-test--callback call :on-error\n                           (format \"unknown mock response: %S\" response))))))\n\n(defun ende-test--fake-llm (responses &optional asynchronous process-factory)\n  \"Return a mock LLM dispatcher for RESPONSES.\nWhen ASYNCHRONOUS is non-nil, calls remain in `ende-test--calls' until\n`ende-test--complete-call' is invoked.  PROCESS-FACTORY can provide a real\nprocess for cancellation tests.\"\n  (setq ende-test--fake-responses (copy-sequence responses))\n  (lambda (messages callbacks &optional options)\n    (push messages ende-test--prompts)\n    (let ((call (list :messages messages\n                      :callbacks callbacks\n                      :options options\n                      :response (pop ende-test--fake-responses))))\n      (when process-factory\n        (setq call\n              (plist-put call :process (funcall process-factory)))\n        (ende-test--callback\n         call :on-start\n         (lambda ()\n           (let ((process (plist-get call :process)))\n             (when (and (processp process) (process-live-p process))\n               (process-put process :ende-cancelled t)\n               (delete-process process))))))\n      (setq ende-test--calls\n            (append ende-test--calls (list call)))\n      (unless asynchronous\n        (ende-test--complete-call call))\n      call)))\n\n(defun ende-test--user-message (messages)\n  \"Return the user content from MESSAGES.\"\n  (cdr (assoc \"user\" messages)))\n\n(defun ende-test--sleep-process ()\n  \"Create a process that remains live until explicitly terminated.\"\n  (make-process :name (generate-new-buffer-name \"ende-test-sleep\")\n                :command (list \"sleep\" \"30\")\n                :connection-type 'pipe\n                :noquery t))\n\n(defun ende-test--select (start end point)\n  \"Activate the region START..END with point at POINT.\"\n  (setq transient-mark-mode t)\n  (goto-char point)\n  (set-mark (if (= point end) start end))\n  (setq mark-active t))\n\n(defun ende-test--count-matches (regexp text)\n  \"Return the number of nonoverlapping REGEXP matches in TEXT.\"\n  (let ((start 0)\n        (count 0))\n    (while (string-match regexp text start)\n      (setq count (1+ count))\n      (setq start (match-end 0)))\n    count))\n\n(defun ende-test--reset ()\n  \"Reset global state and clean test-created processes and buffers.\"\n  (ende-abort-queue)\n  (when (fboundp #'ende-openrouter-batch-abort)\n    (ende-openrouter-batch-abort))\n  (when (buffer-live-p ende-rubber-duck--buffer)\n    (kill-buffer ende-rubber-duck--buffer))\n  (setq ende-rubber-duck--buffer nil)\n  (dolist (call ende-test--calls)\n    (let ((process (plist-get call :process)))\n      (when (and (processp process) (process-live-p process))\n        (delete-process process))))\n  (setq ende-test--prompts nil\n ende-openrouter-batch--next-request-id 0\n        ende-openrouter-batch--known-groups nil\n        ende-summary--next-source-id 0)\n  (when (boundp 'ende-openrouter-batch--groups)\n    (clrhash Output:
{"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende-core.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"6406e3deb7d20a21053105422054b53f91265f9974628898f797b5a380d998c0","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":100,"total_lines":267,"content":";;; ende-core.el --- Core definitions and utilities for ende -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter Samarin\n;; Author: Peter Samarin\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; Package-wide configuration, errors, and generic resource helpers.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'subr-x)\n\n(defgroup ende nil\n  \"Local-LLM text assistance: insert at cursor, improve, summarize.\"\n  :prefix \"ende-\"\n  :group 'convenience)\n\n(defvaralias 'ende-codex-log-tool-diagnostics 'ende-tool-diagnostics)\n\n(defcustom ende-tool-diagnostics t\n  \"Non-nil to retain tool diagnostics in job history for every provider.\n\nDiagnostics show the tool names sent and response item types.  They omit tool\narguments and response content.\"\n  :type 'boolean\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defconst ende-client-name \"ende-mode\"\n  \"Client name advertised in outbound HTTP requests.\")\n\n;;;; Transport and request resource options\n\n(defcustom ende-connect-timeout nil\n  \"Maximum seconds to wait while establishing an LLM connection.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"No connection deadline\" nil)\n                 (number :tag \"Maximum seconds\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-request-timeout nil\n  \"Maximum total seconds allowed for one LLM request.\n\nWhen nil, an active stream has no total wall-clock deadline.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"No total deadline\" nil)\n                 (number :tag \"Maximum seconds\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-response-idle-timeout 60\n  \"Maximum seconds without response-body data before a request fails.\n\nThe wait starts when the curl process starts and resets on each nonempty\nresponse chunk, including SSE heartbeat comments.  Headers alone do not\nreset it.  A server that sends nothing during prompt prefill can therefore\nreach this limit before generating output.  Increase it for slow prefill.\n\nThe value is captured when each request starts; changes affect new requests.\"\n  :type 'number\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-request-chars nil\n  \"Maximum combined characters in all messages sent for one request.\n\nWhen nil, do not impose a request-size limit.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum characters\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-source-name-disclosure t\n  \"Non-nil to include source file or buffer names in operation prompts.\n\nA provider profile can override this setting with its\n`:source-name-disclosure' value.  Source type and major mode are included\nregardless of this setting.\"\n  :type 'boolean\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-output-tokens nil\n  \"Maximum completion tokens requested from an LLM provider.\n\nWhen nil, omit the request limit and let the provider use its own maximum.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Provider default\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum tokens\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-response-bytes (* 256 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum number of response-body bytes accepted from one LLM request.\n\nThe default is deliberately generous so slow or verbose local inference is not\ntreated as an ordinary product limit.  Set it to nil to disable the breaker.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum bytes\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-sse-event-bytes (* 16 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum number of bytes accepted in one SSE line or event.\n\nThe default is deliberately generous.  Set it to nil to disable the breaker.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n","truncated":true}}

{"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"a422d0254b52bbd533233325feb7484ece921caaab319a612ff3676d4caa2875","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":140,"total_lines":140,"content":";;; ende.el --- Local-LLM text assistance for Emacs -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter\n;; Author: Peter\n;; Version: 0.3\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools, llm\n;; Package-Requires: ((emacs \"31.1\"))\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; ende is a minor mode that queues requests to an OpenAI-compatible LLM.\n;;\n;;   C-c e   - ende-selector-open\n;;\n;; TRAMP source buffers are supported: constructed request bodies are sent to\n;; local provider processes.  Generation, provider discovery, Codex model\n;; probing, and OAuth refresh always run curl locally, never on the source\n;; buffer's remote host.  Provider processes and their header/config files use\n;; `temporary-file-directory', which must be an existing absolute local\n;; directory; remote or relative values are rejected.  Refreshed Codex\n;; credentials must also be stored in a local file.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'ende-core)\n(require 'ende-operation)\n(require 'ende-provider)\n(require 'ende-review)\n(require 'ende-project)\n(require 'ende-target)\n(require 'ende-ui)\n(require 'ende-llm)\n(require 'ende-queue)\n(require 'ende-tools)\n(require 'ende-operations)\n(require 'ende-chat)\n(require 'ende-commands)\n(require 'ende-rubber-duck)\n(require 'ende-history)\n(require 'ende-selector)\n\n;;;; Keymap and Minor Mode\n\n(defun ende--make-mode-map ()\n  \"Build the keymap for `ende-mode'.\"\n  (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))\n    (define-key map (kbd \"C-c e\") #'ende-selector-open)\n    map))\n\n(defvar ende-mode-map (ende--make-mode-map) \"Keymap for `ende-mode'.\")\n\n(defvar-local ende--mode-line-string \" ende[0]\"\n  \"Mode-line lighter showing jobs and pending reviews for the current buffer.\")\n\n(defun ende--job-targets-buffer-p (job buffer)\n  \"Return non-nil when active or queued JOB targets BUFFER.\"\n  (and (memq (ende-job-state job) '(queued running))\n       (eq (ende-target-buffer (ende-job-target job)) buffer)))\n\n(defun ende--buffer-job-count (buffer)\n  \"Return the number of active or queued jobs targeting BUFFER.\"\n  (cl-count-if\n   (lambda (job) (ende--job-targets-buffer-p job buffer))\n   (append (ende-active-jobs) (ende-pending-jobs))))\n\n(defun ende--buffer-review-count (buffer)\n  \"Return the number of pending reviews in BUFFER.\"\n  (when (buffer-live-p buffer)\n    (with-current-buffer buffer\n      (save-restriction\n        (widen)\n        (cl-count-if #'ende-review-overlay-p\n                     (overlays-in (point-min) (point-max)))))))\n\n(defun ende--update-mode-line (&optional buffer)\n  \"Update the ende lighter for BUFFER, or the current buffer.\"\n  (let ((buffer (or buffer (current-buffer))))\n    (when (buffer-live-p buffer)\n      (with-current-buffer buffer\n        (let ((jobs (ende--buffer-job-count buffer))\n              (reviews (ende--buffer-review-count buffer)))\n          (setq ende--mode-line-string\n                (if (> reviews 0)\n                    (format \" ende[%d|%d]\" jobs reviews)\n                  (format \" ende[%d]\" jobs))))\n        (force-mode-line-update)))))\n\n(defun ende--mode-line-job-event (job _event)\n  \"Refresh the mode-line count for JOB's target buffer.\"\n  (ende--update-mode-line\n   (ende-target-buffer (ende-job-target job))))\n\n(defun ende--mode-line-review-event (buffer)\n  \"Refresh the mode-line count for reviews in BUFFER.\"\n  (ende--update-mode-line buffer))\n\n(add-hook 'ende-queue-abort-functions #'ende-provider-abort-discovery)\n(add-hook 'ende-queue-abort-functions #'ende-openrouter-batch-abort)\n(add-hook 'ende-queue-abort-functions #'ende-ui-clear-all)\n(add-hook 'ende-job-event-functions #'ende-ui-job-event)\n(add-hook 'ende-job-event-functions #'ende--mode-line-job-event)\n(add-hook 'ende-review-change-functions #'ende--mode-line-review-event)\n\n;;;###autoload\n(define-minor-mode ende-mode\n  \"Minor mode for queueing LLM requests.\"\n  :lighter ende--mode-line-string\n  :keymap ende-mode-map\n  (when ende-mode\n    (ende-model-selection-initialize-buffer))\n  (ende--update-mode-line))\n\n;;;###autoload\n(defun ende-reload ()\n  \"Abort active jobs and reload all ende files in dependency order.\"\n  (interactive)\n  (ende-abort-queue)\n  (let* ((dir (or (file-name-directory (locate-library \"ende.el\"))\n                  (user-error \"ende.el not found on load-path\")))\n         (files '(\"ende-core.el\" \"ende-operation.el\" \"ende-protocol.el\"\n                  \"ende-transport.el\" \"ende-curl.el\" \"ende-provider.el\"\n                  \"ende-codex.el\"\n                  \"ende-review.el\" \"ende-project.el\" \"ende-target.el\"\n                  \"ende-ui.el\"\n                  \"ende-openrouter-batch.el\" \"ende-llm.el\"\n                  \"ende-queue.el\" \"ende-edit.el\"\n                  \"ende-tools.el\"\n                  \"ende-chat-config.el\"\n                  \"ende-summary.el\" \"ende-operations.el\"\n                  \"ende-chat.el\" \"ende-commands.el\" \"ende-rubber-duck.el\"\n                  \"ende-history.el\"\n                  \"ende-selector.el\" \"ende.el\")))\n    (dolist (f files) (load (expand-file-name f dir)))\n    (setq ende-mode-map (ende--make-mode-map))\n    (message \"ende: reloaded from %s\" dir)))\n\n(provide 'ende)\n\n;;; ende.el ends here\n","truncated":null}}

*** Yes—Emacs can run tests conditionally while loading

Emacs Lisp does not have a direct equivalent of Racket’s ~module+ test~, with a separately loadable test submodule. But loading an Elisp file evaluates its top-level forms, so an ordinary conditional can control whether embedded tests are defined and executed.

The important distinction is:

- ~ert-deftest~ /registers/ a test. It does not execute it.
- An ERT runner executes the registered tests.

For example, this could go near the end of ~ende-core.el~, after the functions being tested:

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'ert)

(defvar ende-run-tests-on-load nil
  "Non-nil means execute embedded tests while loading libraries.")

(when ende-run-tests-on-load
  (ert-deftest ende-core-test/plist-merge ()
    (should
     (equal (ende-plist-merge '(:a 1) '(:a 2 :b 3))
            '(:a 2 :b 3))))

  (ert-run-tests-batch "^ende-core-test/"))
#+end_src

Set the variable /before/ loading:

#+begin_src sh
emacs -Q --batch -L . \
  --eval '(setq ende-run-tests-on-load t)' \
  -l ende-core.el
#+end_src

The tests then execute before loading finishes. Some qualifications:

- This minimal version loads ERT unconditionally, also making its macros available during byte compilation. The /tests/ remain conditional.
- A runtime guard does not remove the test code from the compiled library or give it a separate namespace.
- ~require~ normally loads a library only once. Setting the flag after the library has already been required does not trigger anything; use a fresh Emacs process or explicitly reload it.
- ~ert-run-tests-batch~ reports results but does not terminate Emacs with a failure exit status. For CI, that responsibility belongs to a final ~ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit~ call—not one inside every production library.

So: *possible, yes; equivalent to Racket’s test submodules, no.*

*** For this project, I would split the tests into ~tests/~

Your current ~ende-test.el~ is 10,698 lines. Moving all those tests into production files would redistribute the size, but it would also make those source files substantially harder to navigate.

I would start with something like:

#+begin_example
tests/
  ende-test-helpers.el
  ende-core-test.el
  ende-queue-test.el
  ende-provider-test.el
  ende-protocol-test.el
  ende-tools-test.el
  ende-chat-config-test.el
  ende-chat-test.el
  ende-selector-test.el
  ende-integration-test.el
#+end_example

These are suggested boundaries, not a requirement to have exactly one test file per source file. Split large subsystems further by behavior when useful.

The organization should follow a few rules:

- Move shared fixtures and mocks, such as ~ende-test--fake-llm~ and ~ende-test--reset~, into ~ende-test-helpers.el~.
- Keep helpers used by only one suite in that suite.
- Each suite explicitly requires its dependencies. It should not depend on another test file having been loaded first.
- Keep tests spanning several modules together as integration tests rather than forcing them into an arbitrary module’s suite.
- Initially preserve test names and assertions. Make the first change an organizational refactor, not a simultaneous rewrite.

Separate files do not prevent testing internal functions: Emacs Lisp’s ~--~ naming convention does not impose private access restrictions.

There is also a project-specific reason to avoid running tests during each production file’s load: your shared reset helper touches queues, providers, history, chat, and other subsystems. Tests using it need the relevant application pieces initialized. Running them halfway through loading the dependency graph invites load-order problems.

**** Your Makefile already supports this layout

The existing Makefile:

- Finds ~.el~ files recursively.
- Adds their directories to ~load-path~.
- Identifies files ending in ~-test.el~ or ~-tests.el~.
- Loads those files, then invokes ~ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit~.

Therefore, splitting the monolith into files with those suffixes under ~tests/~ should require no fundamental Makefile change. The helper filename above deliberately does not match either test-suite suffix; suites load it with ~require~.

*** Preserve “run at load time” at the test entry point

My preferred compromise is:

*Loading application code does not run tests. Loading the explicit test entry point does.*

That avoids premature execution while still giving you a single load-and-run operation. For example, an optional batch-only entry point:

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; tests/batch-runner.el --- Load and run the test suite

(require 'ert)

(let ((directory (file-name-directory load-file-name)))
  (dolist (file (directory-files directory t "-tests?\\.el\\'"))
    (load file nil nil t)))

(ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit)
#+end_src

Run it with:

#+begin_src sh
emacs -Q --batch -L . -L tests -l tests/batch-runner.el
#+end_src

This executes the suite as part of loading ~batch-runner.el~, with the correct process exit status. Its name intentionally avoids the test-file suffixes, so neither its own discovery loop nor your Makefile mistakes it for a suite. Your existing ~make test~ already provides the equivalent workflow, so this extra entry point is optional.

*My recommendation:* use separate suites under ~tests/~, shared test-only helpers, and one explicit execution boundary. Embedded, opt-in tests are reasonable for small self-contained libraries, but I would not build a custom test-submodule mechanism for this application.

*** Is there an advantage to a batch runner?

It is optional. Its main advantage is providing *one test entry point independent of Make*: the shell, CI, and Make can all invoke the same runner. More elaborate setup—fixtures, environment checks, selectors—can then live in Elisp rather than shell-quoted Makefile expressions.

Yes, you could change that runner without changing the Makefile, *if Make delegates to it*. But your current Makefile already discovers test files recursively, so adding or moving test files does not require updating it anyway.

For your current setup, I would keep the Makefile approach. A batch runner would mostly duplicate logic you already have.

*** Running only one test file

After splitting the tests, your existing Makefile supports this *without modification*:

#+begin_src sh
make test TEST_FILES=tests/ende-queue-test.el
#+end_src

A command-line Make variable overrides the ~TEST_FILES~ assignment in the Makefile.

For several selected files:

#+begin_src sh
make test TEST_FILES='tests/ende-queue-test.el tests/ende-provider-test.el'
#+end_src

Or, without Make:

#+begin_src sh
emacs -Q --batch -L . -L tests \
  -l tests/ende-queue-test.el \
  -f ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit
#+end_src

This loads the selected suite and its dependencies, then runs all registered tests. In a fresh Emacs process, that effectively means just that suite—provided its dependencies are helpers and application libraries, not other test suites.

*** Running specific tests within that file

Load the file as above, but pass an ERT selector instead of running everything. For example, assuming you preserve the existing test names:

#+begin_src sh
emacs -Q --batch -L . -L tests \
  -l tests/ende-queue-test.el \
  --eval '(ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit "^ende-test/queue-")'
#+end_src

A string selector matches test names as a regexp.

For exactly one named test:

#+begin_src sh
emacs -Q --batch -L . -L tests \
  -l tests/ende-queue-test.el \
  --eval '(ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit
           (quote ende-test/queue-snapshots-do-not-expose-list-state))'
#+end_src

The distinction is:

- *Loading files* determines which tests are registered.
- *The ERT selector* determines which registered tests execute.

The batch runner I suggested earlier always discovers and loads every suite. It would need additional argument handling to support selecting files. Your existing Makefile already provides that through ~TEST_FILES~, so there is no need to introduce a runner just for this.

{"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende-queue.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"6b8bbb4f81880463e134b95c487bdd62195ac6b22ecb0f6671bce2983e1df17c","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":100,"total_lines":535,"content":";;; ende-queue.el --- FIFO job queue and execution engine for ende -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter\n;; Author: Peter\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; FIFO queue management, job identity, lifecycle transitions, and process\n;; cancellation for ende.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'ende-core)\n(require 'ende-operation)\n(require 'ende-provider)\n(require 'ende-target)\n\n;;;; Job records\n\n(defun ende--invoke-cancellation (cancel-function)\n  \"Invoke CANCEL-FUNCTION without allowing cleanup failures to escape.\"\n  (when (functionp cancel-function)\n    (ende-ignore-errors cancel-function)))\n\n(cl-defstruct (ende-job (:constructor ende--make-job))\n  \"A queued LLM operation and its asynchronous lifecycle state.\"\n  id type state cancel-function overlay target profile instruction\n  metadata metadata-rank result error structured-state\n  enqueued-at started-at finished-at)\n\n(cl-defstruct (ende-queue-fence (:constructor ende--make-fence))\n  \"A queue barrier for jobs present when the fence was created.\"\n  provider cutoff)\n\n(defvar ende--next-job-id 0\n  \"Next numeric identifier assigned to a job.\")\n\n(defvar ende--current-job nil\n  \"One active job retained for compatibility and status display.\n\nCallbacks must not use this variable to identify their originating job.  It\nis the scheduler's first active job; `ende--active-jobs' is authoritative.\")\n\n(defvar ende--active-jobs nil\n  \"Active jobs, in reverse dispatch order.\")\n\n(defvar ende--fences nil\n  \"Queue barriers that have not yet been crossed by later jobs.\")\n\n(defvar ende--aborting nil\n  \"Non-nil while queue state is being invalidated by an abort.\")\n\n(defvar-local ende--source-buffer-jobs nil\n  \"Jobs queued for or running against the current source buffer.\")\n\n;; Job ownership and cancellation must survive changes of major mode.\n(put 'ende--source-buffer-jobs 'permanent-local t)\n(put 'ende--source-buffer-killed 'permanent-local-hook t)\n\n(defun ende--unregister-source-job (job)\n  \"Remove JOB from its source buffer's job registry.\"\n  (let ((buffer (ende-target-buffer (ende-job-target job))))\n    (when (buffer-live-p buffer)\n      (with-current-buffer buffer\n        (setq ende--source-buffer-jobs\n              (delq job ende--source-buffer-jobs))\n        (when (null ende--source-buffer-jobs)\n          (remove-hook 'kill-buffer-hook\n                       #'ende--source-buffer-killed t)))))\n  job)\n\n(defun ende-job-terminal-p (job)\n  \"Non-nil when JOB has reached a terminal state.\"\n  (memq (ende-job-state job) '(completed failed cancelled skipped)))\n\n(defun ende-job-current-p (job)\n  \"Non-nil when JOB is the active, still-settleable job.\"\n  (and (memq job ende--active-jobs)\n       (eq (ende-job-state job) 'running)\n       (not ende--aborting)))\n\n(defun ende-job-cancellation-set (job cancel-function)\n  \"Associate CANCEL-FUNCTION with JOB, or invoke it if JOB is stale.\"\n  (if (ende-job-current-p job)\n      (setf (ende-job-cancel-function job) cancel-function)\n    (ende--invoke-cancellation cancel-function))\n  cancel-function)\n\n(defun ende--job-release-current (job)\n  \"Release JOB from the active set and release its target.\"\n  (setq ende--active-jobs (delq job ende--active-jobs)\n        ende--current-job (car ende--active-jobs))\n  (ende--unregister-source-job job)\n  (ende-ignore-errors #'ende-target-release (ende-job-target job)))\n\n;;;; Queue state\n\n(defvar ende-queue nil\n","truncated":true}}

A small, natural suite would be *roughly 60–70 lines with two tests*, excluding shared helpers. For example, your existing mode-line tests could become ~tests/ende-mode-line-test.el~.

The absolute smallest depends on the final grouping, but this is a realistic example—not an artificially tiny one-test file:

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; ende-mode-line-test.el --- Mode-line tests -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-

;;; Commentary:

;; Coverage for buffer-local job and pending-review counts.

;;; Code:

(require 'ert)
(require 'ende)
(require 'ende-test-helpers)

(ert-deftest ende-test/mode-line-reports-buffer-job-count ()
  (ende-test--reset)
  (let ((ende-llm-function
         (ende-test--fake-llm (list :stall :stall) t)))
    (ende-test--with-buffer "test"
      (ende-mode 1)
      (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0]"))
      (let ((first (ende-write-anywhere))
            (second nil))
        (should (eq (ende-job-state first) 'running))
        (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[1]"))
        (setq second (ende-write-anywhere))
        (should (eq (ende-job-state second) 'queued))
        (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[2]"))
        (should (string-match-p
                 "Thinking"
                 (ende-test--overlay-label (ende-job-overlay first))))
        (should (string-match-p
                 "Queued"
                 (ende-test--overlay-label (ende-job-overlay second))))
        (ende-abort-queue)
        (should-not (ende-job-overlay first))
        (should-not (ende-job-overlay second))
        (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0]"))))))

(ert-deftest ende-test/mode-line-reports-pending-review-count ()
  (ende-test--reset)
  (ende-test--with-buffer "test"
    (ende-mode 1)
    (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0]"))
    (let ((review (ende-review-create-overlay
                   (current-buffer) 1 2 nil nil "t")))
      (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0|1]"))
      (ende-approve-all-output)
      (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0]"))
      (ende-review-create-overlay
       (current-buffer) 1 2 nil nil "t")
      (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0|1]"))
      (ende-reject-all-output)
      (should (string= ende--mode-line-string " ende[0]"))
      (should-not (ende-review-overlay-p review)))))

(provide 'ende-mode-line-test)

;;; ende-mode-line-test.el ends here
#+end_src

This assumes the existing ~ende-test--reset~, ~ende-test--with-buffer~, mock LLM, and related helpers have been moved into ~tests/ende-test-helpers.el~, which provides ~ende-test-helpers~. The test names and assertions above are preserved; only their location and indentation change.

After that split, run just this file with:

#+begin_src sh
make test TEST_FILES=tests/ende-mode-line-test.el
#+end_src

That is the entire suite file: dependencies, two tests, and a footer. No discovery logic or runner needs to be repeated in it.

?= emacs\nEMACSCLIENT ?= emacsclient\n\n.DEFAULT_GOAL := check\n\n# All project Elisp files, excluding Git internals.\nEL_FILES := $(sort $(shell find . -type d -name .git -prune -o \\\n\t-type f -name '*.el' -print))\nEL_DIRS := $(sort $(dir $(EL_FILES)))\nTEST_FILES := $(filter %-test.el %-tests.el,$(EL_FILES))\n\nEMACS_BATCH = $(EMACS) -Q --batch \\\n\t$(foreach dir,$(EL_DIRS),-L \"$(dir)\") \\\n\t--eval '(setq load-prefer-newer t)'\n\n\n# These expressions must not contain shell single quotes.\ndefine CHECK_SYNTAX\n(let ((files command-line-args-left))\n  (setq command-line-args-left nil)\n  (dolist (file files)\n    (message \"Checking %s\" file)\n    (with-temp-buffer\n      (insert-file-contents file)\n      (emacs-lisp-mode)\n      (check-parens))))\nendef\n\n# Use absolute paths: the server may have a different working directory.\ndefine INDENT_FILES\n(let ((files (quote ($(foreach file,$(EL_FILES),\"$(abspath $(file))\")))))\n  (save-current-buffer\n    (dolist (file files)\n      (message \"Indenting %s\" file)\n      (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect file)\n        (save-excursion\n          (save-restriction\n            (widen)\n            (indent-region (point-min) (point-max))))\n        (save-buffer))))\n  (format \"Indented %d files\" (length files)))\nendef\n\n.PHONY: check lint syntax compile test format clean\n\n# Explicit sequencing also keeps \"make -j check\" safe.\ncheck:\n\t$(MAKE) clean\n\t$(MAKE) lint\n\t$(MAKE) test\n\nclean:\n\t$(RM) $(EL_FILES:.el=.elc)\n\ncompile:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) \\\n\t\t--eval '(setq byte-compile-error-on-warn t)' \\\n\t\t-f batch-byte-compile $(EL_FILES)\nformat:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) --eval '$(strip $(INDENT_FILES))'\n\nlint:\n\t$(MAKE) syntax\n\t$(MAKE) compile\n\nsyntax:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) --eval '$(strip $(CHECK_SYNTAX))' $(EL_FILES)\n\ntest:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) \\\n\t\t$(foreach file,$(TEST_FILES),-l $(file)) \\\n\t\t-f ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit\n","truncated":null}}

*** Objective

Split the existing ~ende-test.el~ into cohesive ERT suites under ~tests/~, preserving coverage and behavior.

This is an *organizational refactor*, not a test rewrite. Keep the current Makefile-based execution model. Do not introduce embedded tests, a batch runner, or a new testing framework.

*** Ground rules

- Preserve existing test names, assertions, fixtures, mock responses, tags, and expected results.
- Do not change production code to accommodate the split.
- Do not add narrow tests asserting filenames, directory structure, or suite counts. Use temporary migration checks instead.
- Keep ~lexical-binding: t~ in every new Elisp file.
- Preserve ~defvar~ declarations: they matter for dynamically binding mock state in lexically bound tests.
- Suites register tests when loaded; they do not call an ERT runner themselves.
- Shared helpers must not register or execute tests.
- No suite may require another suite. Dependencies must point to application libraries or helper libraries.
- Prefer straightforward, idiomatic Elisp and explicit ~require~ forms. Do not invent abstractions merely to reduce a few repeated lines.
- Preserve literal fixture strings exactly. Reindent moved code if needed, but do not reformat unrelated files.
- Commit coherent, verified changes as you go. Stage only this task’s changes.

*** Proposed suite boundaries

Read the entire monolith before moving anything. Use this as a starting map, not a requirement to manufacture files without existing coverage.

| Suite filename under ~tests/~           | Main responsibility                                      |
|----------------------------------------+----------------------------------------------------------|
| ~ende-tools-test.el~                    | Project tools, file/buffer access, scope enforcement      |
| ~ende-target-test.el~                   | Target capture, source metadata, conflict detection       |
| ~ende-edit-test.el~                     | Structured edits, anchors, reconciliation and repair      |
| ~ende-operations-test.el~               | Insert/improve operations, prompts and result application |
| ~ende-summary-test.el~                  | Summaries, citations and source links                     |
| ~ende-review-test.el~                   | Approval, rejection, overlays and highlighting            |
| ~ende-history-test.el~                  | History recording and display                            |
| ~ende-ui-test.el~                       | Progress/status overlays and cleanup                     |
| ~ende-mode-line-test.el~                | The two existing mode-line count tests                    |
| ~ende-queue-test.el~                    | FIFO ordering, lifecycle, cancellation and stale callbacks |
| ~ende-queue-parallel-test.el~           | Concurrency limits, parallel scheduling and fences        |
| ~ende-protocol-test.el~                 | Incremental protocol and reasoning parsers                |
| ~ende-transport-test.el~                | Default transport, HTTP/SSE handling and resource limits  |
| ~ende-provider-test.el~                 | Profiles, configuration and runtime settings              |
| ~ende-provider-discovery-test.el~       | Discovery, caching, model metadata and reasoning efforts  |
| ~ende-codex-test.el~                    | OpenAI Responses, OAuth, quota and model probing           |
| ~ende-openrouter-batch-test.el~         | Batch submission, polling, results and cancellation       |
| ~ende-selector-test.el~                | Provider/model/effort selection and selector actions      |
| ~ende-chat-config-test.el~              | Configuration parsing, inheritance and permissions        |
| ~ende-chat-context-test.el~             | Context traversal, roles, visibility and saved history     |
| ~ende-chat-output-test.el~              | Reply formatting, rendering and Org structure             |
| ~ende-chat-tools-test.el~               | Tool dispatch loops and authorization integration        |
| ~ende-chat-actions-test.el~             | Ask/compact actions and asynchronous lifecycle            |

Routing rules:

- Group by the behavior under test, not every function referenced. A queue test that uses a provider still belongs with queue tests.
- Cross-module tests may stay in the suite for the feature they exercise. Do not dump them all into a miscellaneous integration file.
- Split an oversized suite further by a meaningful behavior boundary. Do not balance files mechanically by line count.
- Preserve the original relative order of tests within each resulting suite.
- Adjust this map if the inventory reveals better boundaries.

*** Implementation sequence

**** 1. Establish the baseline

Before editing:

1. Inspect ~git status~ and preserve unrelated work.
2. Run ~make check~ using the project’s supported Emacs version.
3. Record the test names, total count, and pass/fail/skip results.
4. Save the original ~ende-test.el~ outside the repository for comparison. A backup ~.el~ file inside the project would be discovered by the Makefile.
5. Inventory /every top-level form/: tests, functions, macros, variables, constants, requires, and any setup forms.

Do not assume that all helpers are in the initial “Test harness” section. For example, ~ende-test--with-buffer~ appears much later, and domain-specific helpers are interspersed with tests.

If the baseline fails, report the failure before proceeding. Do not silently fix unrelated bugs or weaken tests.

**** 2. Extract shared helpers first

Create ~tests/ende-test-helpers.el~ and move the common harness into it:

- Mock state variables.
- Callback and fake-LLM helpers.
- Shared buffer/region utilities.
- ~ende-test--reset~.
- Other helpers genuinely used by multiple suites.

For this first extraction, preserve the original dependency setup, including the application-wide ~require~ forms. The reset helper accesses many subsystems; trying to minimize dependencies simultaneously would introduce unnecessary risk.

Replace those definitions in the monolith with:

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'ende-test-helpers)
#+end_src

Keep helper names and implementations unchanged.

Helpers used by only one suite should eventually live in that suite. If several chat suites need the same chat helpers, use ~ende-chat-test-helpers.el~, requiring the common helper library. Avoid creating many tiny helper libraries.

All helper filenames must avoid the ~-test.el~ and ~-tests.el~ suffixes.

Run ~make check~ and compare the test inventory before committing.

Suggested commit: =Extract shared test helpers=

**** 3. Move complete groups incrementally

Start with the two mode-line tests as a small pilot. Confirm that the new suite runs independently, then move the remaining groups in coherent batches.

Each suite should have:

- A normal file header and short commentary.

#+begin_src markdown
# Objective

Split the existing `ende-test.el` into cohesive ERT suites under `tests/`, preserving coverage and behavior.

This is an **organizational refactor**, not a test rewrite. Keep the current Makefile-based execution model. Do not introduce embedded tests, a batch runner, or a new testing framework.

# Ground rules

- Preserve existing test names, assertions, fixtures, mock responses, tags, and expected results.
- Do not change production code to accommodate the split.
- Do not add narrow tests asserting filenames, directory structure, or suite counts. Use temporary migration checks instead.
- Keep `lexical-binding: t` in every new Elisp file.
- Preserve `defvar` declarations: they matter for dynamically binding mock state in lexically bound tests.
- Suites register tests when loaded; they do not call an ERT runner themselves.
- Shared helpers must not register or execute tests.
- No suite may require another suite. Dependencies must point to application libraries or helper libraries.
- Prefer straightforward, idiomatic Elisp and explicit `require` forms. Do not invent abstractions merely to reduce a few repeated lines.
- Preserve literal fixture strings exactly. Reindent moved code if needed, but do not reformat unrelated files.
- Commit coherent, verified changes as you go. Stage only this task’s changes.

# Proposed suite boundaries

Read the entire monolith before moving anything. Use this as a starting map, not a requirement to manufacture files without existing coverage.

| Suite filename under `tests/` | Main responsibility |
|---|---|
| `ende-tools-test.el` | Project tools, file/buffer access, scope enforcement |
| `ende-target-test.el` | Target capture, source metadata, conflict detection |
| `ende-edit-test.el` | Structured edits, anchors, reconciliation and repair |
| `ende-operations-test.el` | Insert/improve operations, prompts and result application |
| `ende-summary-test.el` | Summaries, citations and source links |
| `ende-review-test.el` | Approval, rejection, overlays and highlighting |
| `ende-history-test.el` | History recording and display |
| `ende-ui-test.el` | Progress/status overlays and cleanup |
| `ende-mode-line-test.el` | The two existing mode-line count tests |
| `ende-queue-test.el` | FIFO ordering, lifecycle, cancellation and stale callbacks |
| `ende-queue-parallel-test.el` | Concurrency limits, parallel scheduling and fences |
| `ende-protocol-test.el` | Incremental protocol and reasoning parsers |
| `ende-transport-test.el` | Default transport, HTTP/SSE handling and resource limits |
| `ende-provider-test.el` | Profiles, configuration and runtime settings |
| `ende-provider-discovery-test.el` | Discovery, caching, model metadata and reasoning efforts |
| `ende-codex-test.el` | OpenAI Responses, OAuth, quota and model probing |
| `ende-openrouter-batch-test.el` | Batch submission, polling, results and cancellation |
| `ende-selector-test.el` | Provider/model/effort selection and selector actions |
| `ende-chat-config-test.el` | Configuration parsing, inheritance and permissions |
| `ende-chat-context-test.el` | Context traversal, roles, visibility and saved history |
| `ende-chat-output-test.el` | Reply formatting, rendering and Org structure |
| `ende-chat-tools-test.el` | Tool dispatch loops and authorization integration |
| `ende-chat-actions-test.el` | Ask/compact actions and asynchronous lifecycle |

Routing rules:

- Group by the behavior under test, not every function referenced. A queue test that uses a provider still belongs with queue tests.
- Cross-module tests may stay in the suite for the feature they exercise. Do not dump them all into a miscellaneous integration file.
- Split an oversized suite further by a meaningful behavior boundary. Do not balance files mechanically by line count.
- Preserve the original relative order of tests within each resulting suite.
- Adjust this map if the inventory reveals better boundaries.

# Implementation sequence

## 1. Establish the baseline

Before editing:

1. Inspect `git status` and preserve unrelated work.
2. Run `make check` using the project’s supported Emacs version.
3. Record the test names, total count, and pass/fail/skip results.
4. Save the original `ende-test.el` outside the repository for comparison. A backup `.el` file inside the project would be discovered by the Makefile.
5. Inventory *every top-level form*: tests, functions, macros, variables, constants, requires, and any setup forms.

Do not assume that all helpers are in the initial “Test harness” section. For example, `ende-test--with-buffer` appears much later, and domain-specific helpers are interspersed with tests.

If the baseline fails, report the failure before proceeding. Do not silently fix unrelated bugs or weaken tests.

## 2. Extract shared helpers first

Create `tests/ende-test-helpers.el` and move the common harness into it:

- Mock state variables.
- Callback and fake-LLM helpers.
- Shared buffer/region utilities.
- `ende-test--reset`.
- Other helpers genuinely used by multiple suites.

For this first extraction, preserve the original dependency setup, including the application-wide `require` forms. The reset helper accesses many subsystems; trying to minimize dependencies simultaneously would introduce unnecessary risk.

Replace those definitions in the monolith with:

```emacs-lisp
(require 'ende-test-helpers)
```

Keep helper names and implementations unchanged.

Helpers used by only one suite should eventually live in that suite. If several chat suites need the same chat helpers, use `ende-chat-test-helpers.el`, requiring the common helper library. Avoid creating many tiny helper libraries.

All helper filenames must avoid the `-test.el` and `-tests.el` suffixes.

Run `make check` and compare the test inventory before committing.

Suggested commit: `Extract shared test helpers`

## 3. Move complete groups incrementally

Start with the two mode-line tests as a small pilot. Confirm that the new suite runs independently, then move the remaining groups in coherent batches.

Each suite should have:

- A normal file header and short commentary.
- Explicit dependencies before any helper or test definitions.
- Suite-local helpers, followed by tests.
- A matching `provide` form and footer.

For suites using the shared application harness, this is a suitable starting structure:

```emacs-lisp
;;; ende-mode-line-test.el --- Mode-line tests -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-

;;; Commentary:

;; Coverage for buffer-local job and pending-review counts.

;;; Code:

(require 'ert)
(require 'ende)
(require 'ende-test-helpers)

;; Existing tests move here unchanged.

(provide 'ende-mode-line-test)

;;; ende-mode-line-test.el ends here
```

Add explicit `cl-lib`, `json`, or other requires where the suite uses them. Retaining the broad `require 'ende` during this refactor is acceptable; dependency optimization is a separate task.

For each move:

1. Move whole top-level forms, including relevant comments.
2. Remove the moved forms from the monolith in the same change.
3. Run the extracted suite alone.
4. Run `make check`.
5. Confirm that no tests or helpers were lost, duplicated, or altered.

Do not split the file using regex matches or fixed line ranges alone. Fixture strings contain text that resembles code and Org structure. If automating extraction, use the Emacs Lisp reader to find complete forms and preserve their original source text.

Suggested subsequent commits:

- `Split editor test suites`
- `Split queue and transport tests`
- `Split provider test suites`
- `Split chat test suites`

Adjust the batches as needed; each commit should leave the repository testable.

## 4. Retire the monolith

Once every form has an appropriate home:

- Delete `ende-test.el`.
- Remove the obsolete `ende-test.elc`, if present. After deleting the source, the current `make clean` no longer derives that compiled filename.
- Search documentation, CI, scripts, and Elisp for references to the old filename or `ende-test` feature. Update relevant references.
- Do not retain an aggregate `ende-test.el` that loads the new suites: the Makefile would discover both it and the suites, potentially registering tests twice.
- Leave the Makefile unchanged unless a concrete incompatibility is found. It already discovers nested suites and adds their directories to `load-path`.

Document these commands briefly in the existing development documentation, or in `tests/README.org` if there is no suitable location:

```sh
make test
make test TEST_FILES=tests/ende-mode-line-test.el
make test TEST_FILES='tests/ende-queue-test.el tests/ende-provider-test.el'
```

Suggested commit: `Finish test suite split`

# Verification requirements

## Check preservation, not just successful execution

Use a temporary audit outside the repository:

- Read the original and resulting files as Lisp forms.
- Collect all `ert-deftest` definitions.
- Reject duplicate names *before* putting them into a name-keyed map.
- Compare the original and final name sets and definition counts.
- Compare corresponding test forms with `equal`. Changes in comments or indentation are acceptable; changed assertions or fixture strings are not.
- Similarly account for every original helper definition and other top-level setup form.

An ERT registry count alone is insufficient: registering the same test name twice can replace the earlier definition and hide a duplicate.

During intermediate commits, compare the union of the remaining monolith and extracted suites with the baseline.

## Test independent loading and compilation

After completing the split, run:

```sh
set -eu

# Source-loaded application and helper libraries.
make clean
make test

for suite in tests/*-test.el tests/*-tests.el; do
    [ -f "$suite" ] || continue
    make test TEST_FILES="$suite"
done

# Syntax checks and byte compilation with warnings treated as errors.
make check

# Execute each compiled suite as well.
for suite in tests/*-test.el tests/*-tests.el; do
    [ -f "$suite" ] || continue
    make test TEST_FILES="${suite}c"
done

git diff --check
```

Also load all suites in reverse filename order once in a fresh Emacs process and run them. This checks for hidden *load-order* dependencies.

If a standalone suite fails:

1. First check missing requires, misplaced helpers, and lost `defvar` declarations.
2. Do not fix it by requiring another suite or enforcing a special runner order.
3. If an existing test genuinely depends on state left by another test, isolate that state with a minimal fixture change.
4. Keep any necessary fixture repair separate from the mechanical move, explain the cause, and preserve the assertions and tested behavior.

Do not disable compiler warnings, skip failing tests, or change expected results to obtain a green run.

# Completion report

Finish with a short report containing:

- The suite layout and helper libraries created.
- Confirmation that all original tests are present exactly once.
- Validation commands and their results.
- Any necessary fixture changes or known baseline failures.
- The short commit messages.

The desired outcome is a boring, readable, verifiable split: the same tests, better organized, and independently runnable.

Configuration is never evaluated as Emacs Lisp.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'org-element)\n(require 'subr-x)\n(require 'ende-project)\n(require 'ende-tools)\n\n(defvar ende-chat-config--block-line nil\n  \"Line of the configuration block currently being applied, if any.\")\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--error (format-string &rest arguments)\n  \"Signal a configuration error formatted with FORMAT-STRING and ARGUMENTS.\"\n  (error \"ende chat config: %s%s\"\n         (apply #'format format-string arguments)\n         (if ende-chat-config--block-line\n             (format \" (block at line %d)\" ende-chat-config--block-line)\n           \"\")))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--reject-reader-dispatch (source)\n  \"Reject Emacs reader dispatch syntax in SOURCE, outside strings/comments.\"\n  (let ((index 0)\n        (length (length source))\n        (in-string nil)\n        (escaped nil)\n        (in-comment nil))\n    (while (< index length)\n      (let ((character (aref source index)))\n        (cond\n         (in-comment\n          (when (= character ?\\n)\n            (setq in-comment nil)))\n         (in-string\n          (cond\n           (escaped (setq escaped nil))\n           ((= character ?\\\\) (setq escaped t))\n           ((= character ?\\\") (setq in-string nil))))\n         ((= character ?\\;) (setq in-comment t))\n         ((= character ?\\\") (setq in-string t))\n         ((= character ?#)\n          (ende-chat-config--error\n           \"reader dispatch syntax is not allowed\")))\n        (setq index (1+ index))))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--skip-space (source position)\n  \"Return the first non-whitespace, non-comment position in SOURCE.\"\n  (let ((length (length source))\n        done)\n    (while (and (< position length) (not done))\n      (cond\n       ((memq (aref source position) '(?\\s ?\\t ?\\r ?\\n))\n        (setq position (1+ position)))\n       ((= (aref source position) ?\\;)\n        (setq position\n              (or (string-match \"\\n\" source position) length)))\n       (t (setq done t))))\n    position))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--read-forms (source)\n  \"Read data forms from SOURCE without Emacs reader dispatch syntax.\"\n  (ende-chat-config--reject-reader-dispatch source)\n  (let ((position 0)\n        forms)\n    (setq position (ende-chat-config--skip-space source position))\n    (while (< position (length source))\n      (condition-case caught\n          (let* ((parsed (read-from-string source position))\n                 (form (car parsed))\n                 (end (cdr parsed)))\n            (unless (> end position)\n              (ende-chat-config--error \"could not read configuration\"))\n            (push form forms)\n            (setq position (ende-chat-config--skip-space source end)))\n        (end-of-file\n         (signal (car caught) (cdr caught)))\n        (error\n         (ende-chat-config--error \"%s\" (error-message-string caught)))))\n    (nreverse forms)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--proper-list-p (value)\n  \"Return non-nil when VALUE is a proper list.\"\n  (and (listp value) (proper-list-p value)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--name (value context)\n  \"Validate and return symbolic VALUE used as a name in CONTEXT.\"\n  (unless (and (symbolp value)\n               (not (keywordp value))\n               (string-match-p \"\\\\`[[:alnum:]_-]+\\\\'\"\n                               (symbol-name value)))\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"invalid %s name: %S\" context value))\n  value)\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--tool-name (value)\n  \"Validate and return registered tool VALUE.\"\n  (let ((name (ende-chat-config--name value \"tool\")))\n    (unless (ende-tool--find name)\n      (ende-chat-config--error \"unknown tool: %s\" name))\n    name))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--pattern (value resource)\n  \"Validate resource path pattern VALUE for RESOURCE.\"\n  (unless (and (stringp value)\n               (not (string-empty-p value))\n               (not (file-name-absolute-p value))\n               (not (member \"..\" (split-string value \"/\" t))))\n    (ende-chat-config--error\n     \"invalid path pattern in resource %s: %S\" resource value))\n  value)\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--local-blocks (tree)\n  \"Map Org nodes in TREE to their direct `ende' configuration blocks.\"\n  (let ((blocks (make-hash-table :test #'eq)))\n    (org-element-map tree 'special-block\n\t\t     (lambda (block)\n\t\t       (let* ((section (org-element-property :parent block))\n\t\t\t      (node (and (eq (org-element-type section) 'section)\n\t\t\t\t\t (org-element-property :parent section))))\n\t\t\t (when (and node\n\t\t\t\t    (memq (org-element-type node) blocks))))\n\t\t     nil nil nil)\n    blocks))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--block-source (block)\n  \"Return the body text of Org configuration BLOCK.\"\n  (let ((begin (org-element-property :contents-begin block))\n        (end (org-element-property :contents-end block)))\n    (if (and begin end)\n        (buffer-substring-no-properties begin end)\n      \"\")))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--copy (configuration)\n  \"Return an independent copy of CONFIGURATION.\"\n  (copy-tree configuration))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--lookup (name definitions)\n  \"Return NAME's definition in alist DEFINITIONS.\"\n  (cdr (assq name definitions)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--put (name value definitions)\n  \"Set NAME to VALUE in DEFINITIONS and return the updated alist.\"\n  (if (assq name definitions)\n      (cons (cons name value) (assq-delete-all name definitions))\n    (cons (cons name value) definitions)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--canonical-root (path)\n  \"Expand and canonicalize existing directory PATH.\"\n  (unless (stringp path)\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"root must be a string\"))\n  (let ((expanded (expand-file-name path)))\n    (unless (file-directory-p expanded)\n      (ende-chat-config--error \"root is not an existing directory: %s\" path))\n    (ende-project-normalize-root expanded)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--remove-grants (predicate configuration)\n  \"Remove grants matching PREDICATE from CONFIGURATION.\nGrants are (RESOURCE . TARGET) pairs, keyed by tool.\"\n  (let (assignments)\n    (dolist (entry (plist-get configuration :grants))\n      (let ((remaining (cl-remove-if predicate (cdr entry))))\n        (when remaining\n          (push (cons (car entry) remaining) assignments))))\n    (plist-put configuration :grants (nreverse assignments))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--define-group (form configuration)\n  \"Install group FORM in CONFIGURATION, revoking its inherited permissions.\"\n  (unless (and (ende-chat-config--proper-list-p form) (>= (length form) 3))\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"malformed group definition: %S\" form))\n  (let* ((name (ende-chat-config--name (nth 1 form) \"group\"))\n         (groups (plist-get configuration :groups))\n         (tools (delete-dups\n                 (mapcar #'ende-chat-config--tool-name (cddr form)))))\n    (when (ende-tool--find name)\n      (ende-chat-config--error \"group name conflicts with registered tool: %s\"\n                               name))\n    (when (assq name groups)\n      (setq configuration\n            (plist-put configuration :active-targets\n                       (remq name (plist-get configuration :active-targets)))\n            configuration\n            (ende-chat-config--remove-grants\n             (lambda (grant) (eq (cdr grant) name)) configuration)))\n    (plist-put configuration :groups\n               (ende-chat-config--put name tools groups))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--define-resource (form configuration)\n  \"Install resource FORM in CONFIGURATION, revoking its inherited grants.\"\n  (unless (and (ende-chat-config--proper-list-p form) (>= (length form) 3))\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"malformed resource definition: %S\" form))\n  (let* ((name (ende-chat-config--name (nth 1 form) \"resource\"))\n         (resources (plist-get configuration :resources))\n         includes excludes)\n    (dolist (rule (cddr form))\n      (unless (and (ende-chat-config--proper-list-p rule)\n                   (= (length rule) 2)\n                   (memq (car rule) '(include exclude)))\n        (ende-chat-config--error \"malformed rule in resource %s: %S\" name rule))\n      (let ((pattern (ende-chat-config--pattern (cadr rule) name)))\n        (if (eq (car rule) 'include)\n            (push pattern includes)\n          (push pattern excludes))))\n    (unless includes\n      (ende-chat-config--error \"resource %s needs an explicit include\" name))\n    (setq includes (nreverse includes)\n          excludes (nreverse excludes))\n    (when (assq name resources)\n      (let ((revoked (plist-get configuration :revoked-resources)))\n        (dolist (entry (plist-get configuration :grants))\n          (when (assq name (cdr entry))\n            (setq revoked\n                  (ende-chat-config--put\n                   (car entry)\n                   (cl-adjoin name (cdr (assq (car entry) revoked)))\n                   revoked))))\n        (setq configuration\n              (plist-put configuration :revoked-resources revoked))))\n    (setq configuration\n          (ende-chat-config--remove-grants\n           (lambda (grant) (eq (car grant) name)) configuration))\n    (plist-put configuration :resources\n               (ende-chat-config--put\n                name (list :include includes :exclude excludes\n                           :include-regexps\n                           (mapcar #'wildcard-to-regexp includes)\n                           :exclude-regexps\n                           (mapcar #'wildcard-to-regexp excludes))\n                resources))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--target-tools (name configuration)\n  \"Return the tools selected by group or tool NAME in CONFIGURATION.\"\n  (let ((name (ende-chat-config--name name \"tool or group\")))\n    (or (ende-chat-config--lookup name (plist-get configuration :groups))\n        (list (ende-chat-config--tool-name name)))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--resolve-tools (names configuration)\n  \"Expand tool or group NAMES using CONFIGURATION's group definitions.\"\n  (let (tools)\n    (dolist (name names)\n      (dolist (tool (ende-chat-config--target-tools name configuration))\n        (push tool tools)))\n    (delete-dups (nreverse tools))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--resource-names (names configuration)\n  \"Validate resource NAMES against {"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende-tools.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"739f69cb3912c144dcc0172b4a2019e36117e47d07846386e488588bf10b2414","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":280,"total_lines":992,"content":";;; ende-tools.el --- Project tools for ende agents -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter\n;; Author: Peter\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools, llm\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; A provider-independent registry and implementation of project-scoped read\n;; tools, disk-file edits, and file creation.  Path-based tools use disk\n;; contents; buffer tools explicitly use visiting-buffer contents.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'subr-x)\n(require 'ende-core)\n(require 'ende-edit)\n(require 'ende-project)\n\n(defcustom ende-tools-max-listed-files 200\n  \"Maximum files returned by one `list_project_files' call.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-tools-max-read-lines 500\n  \"Maximum lines returned by one file or buffer read call.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-tools-max-search-files 1000\n  \"Maximum files inspected by one `search_project' call.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-tools-max-search-results 100\n  \"Maximum matches returned by one `search_project' call.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-tools-max-search-context-lines 3\n  \"Maximum context lines returned around one search match.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-tools-max-edits 100\n  \"Maximum edits accepted by one `edit_file' call.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n;;;; Tool registry\n\n(cl-defstruct (ende-tool (:constructor ende-tool--create))\n  \"One model-callable ende tool.\"\n  name description parameters handler read-only requires-resource)\n\n(defvar ende-tools--registry nil\n  \"Registered model tools, keyed by their symbolic names.\")\n\n(defvar ende-tools--access-config nil\n  \"Dynamically bound resolved chat access configuration for a tool call.\")\n\n(defvar ende-tools--access-tool nil\n  \"Tool symbol currently being authorized by a handler.\")\n\n(defun ende-tool-register\n    (name description parameters handler &optional read-only resource-policy)\n  \"Register tool NAME with DESCRIPTION, PARAMETERS, and HANDLER.\n\nHANDLER receives an arguments object and a canonical project root.  NAME is a\nsymbol internally and is exposed to model transports as a string.  Pass\n`:write' as READ-ONLY for a mutating tool.  RESOURCE-POLICY may be\n`:no-resource' for tools that do not access project paths.\"\n  (unless (and (symbolp name) (functionp handler))\n    (error \"invalid ende tool registration: %s\" name))\n  (let ((tool (ende-tool--create\n               :name name :description description :parameters parameters\n               :handler handler :read-only (not (eq read-only :write))\n               :requires-resource (not (eq resource-policy :no-resource)))))\n    (setf (alist-get name ende-tools--registry) tool)\n    tool))\n\n(defun ende-tool--find (name)\n  \"Return the registered tool called NAME, or nil.\"\n  (let ((name (if (symbolp name) (symbol-name name) (format \"%s\" name))))\n    (cdr (cl-find-if\n          (lambda (entry)\n            (string= name (symbol-name (car entry))))\n\t  ende-tools--registry))))\n\n(defun ende-tool-definitions ()\n  \"Return registered tools in OpenAI-compatible function-tool shape.\"\n  (mapcar\n   (lambda (tool)\n     (list (cons :type \"function\")\n           (cons :function\n                 (list (cons :name (symbol-name (ende-tool-name tool)))\n                       (cons :description (ende-tool-description tool))\n                       (cons :parameters (ende-tool-parameters tool))))))\n   (mapcar #'cdr ende-tools--registry)))\n\n(defun ende-tool-call (name arguments &optional root access-config)\n  \"Invoke model tool NAME with ARGUMENTS within project ROOT.\n\nReturn a JSON-compatible result object.  Tool failures are returned as data so\nan agent can recover from an invalid path, regexp, or stale source version.\nWhen ACCESS-CONFIG is non-nil, it must be the normalized chat configuration\nand controls tool activation and path authorization.\"\n  (let ((tool (ende-tool--find name)))\n    (if (null tool)\n        (list :ok nil :tool (format \"%s\" name)\n              :error (format \"unknown ende tool: %s\" name))\n      (condition-case caught\n          (let ((tool-name (ende-tool-name tool)))\n            (when access-config\n              (unless (plist-get access-config :root)\n                (error \"chat tool configuration has no explicit root\"))\n              (unless (memq tool-name (plist-get access-config :active-tools))\n                (error \"tool is not active in this chat: %s\" tool-name))\n              (when (and (ende-tool-requires-resource tool)\n                         (null (cdr (assq\n                                     tool-name\n                                     (plist-get access-config\n                                                :tool-resources)))))\n                (error \"tool has no assigned resources: %s\" tool-name)))\n            (let* ((root (ende-project-normalize-root\n                          (or (and access-config\n                                   (plist-get access-config :root))\n                              root\n                              (ende-project-root))))\n                   (ende-tools--access-config access-config)\n                   (ende-tools--access-tool tool-name))\n              (list :ok t\n                    :tool (symbol-name tool-name)\n                    :result (funcall (ende-tool-handler tool) arguments root))))\n        ((error quit)\n         (list :ok nil\n               :tool (symbol-name (ende-tool-name tool))\n               :error (error-message-string caught)))))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--resource-allows-p (relative resource)\n  \"Return non-nil when RESOURCE permits project-relative path RELATIVE.\"\n  (and (cl-some (lambda (regexp) (string-match-p regexp relative))\n                (plist-get resource :include-regexps))\n       (not (cl-some (lambda (regexp) (string-match-p regexp relative))\n                     (plist-get resource :exclude-regexps)))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--path-authorized-p (path root)\n  \"Return non-nil when canonical PATH is allowed by the current tool policy.\"\n  (if (null ende-tools--access-config)\n      t\n    (let* ((root (file-name-as-directory root))\n           (canonical\n            (condition-case nil\n                (if (file-exists-p path)\n                    (file-truename path)\n                  (expand-file-name path root))\n              (error nil)))\n           (relative (and canonical\n                          (file-in-directory-p canonical root)\n                          (file-relative-name canonical root)))\n           (assignments (plist-get ende-tools--access-config\n                                   :tool-resources))\n           (resource-names (cdr (assq ende-tools--access-tool assignments)))\n           (resources (plist-get ende-tools--access-config :resources)))\n      (and relative\n           (not (ende-project--excluded-p relative))\n           (cl-some\n            (lambda (name)\n              (let ((resource (cdr (assq name resources))))\n                (and resource\n                     (ende-tools--resource-allows-p relative resource))))\n            resource-names)))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--assert-path-authorized (path root)\n  \"Signal unless PATH is allowed for the current tool in ROOT.\"\n  (unless (ende-tools--path-authorized-p path root)\n    (error \"path is not authorized by this chat's resources: %s\"\n           (file-relative-name path root)))\n  path)\n\n;;;; Arguments and source resolution\n\n(defun ende-tools--argument (arguments name)\n  \"Return NAME from JSON hash table, plist, or alist ARGUMENTS.\"\n  (cond\n   ((hash-table-p arguments)\n    (gethash name arguments))\n   ((listp arguments)\n    (or (plist-get arguments (intern (concat \":\" name)))\n        (alist-get name arguments nil nil #'equal)\n        (alist-get (intern name) arguments)))\n   (t nil)))\n\n(defun ende-tools--required-string (arguments name)\n  \"Return required string argument NAME from ARGUMENTS.\"\n  (let ((value (ende-tools--argument arguments name)))\n    (unless (and (stringp value) (not (string-empty-p value)))\n      (error \"tool argument %s must be a non-empty string\" name))\n    value))\n\n(defun ende-tools--bounded-integer\n    (arguments name default maximum &optional minimum)\n  \"Return integer NAME from ARGUMENTS bounded by MINIMUM and MAXIMUM.\"\n  (let ((value (or (ende-tools--argument arguments name) default))\n        (minimum (or minimum 1)))\n    (unless (and (integerp value) (>= value minimum) (<= value maximum))\n      (error \"tool argument %s must be an integer from %d to %d\"\n             name minimum maximum))\n    value))\n\n(defun ende-tools--resolve-source (arguments root)\n  \"Resolve one path or buffer_id in ARGUMENTS below ROOT.\n\nReturn a plist containing `:kind', `:path', `:relative', and optionally\n`:buffer'.  This resolver is for buffer-oriented access; path-based file tools\nuse `ende-tools--resolve-disk-source' instead.\"\n  (let* ((path (ende-tools--argument arguments \"path\"))\n         (buffer-id (ende-tools--argument arguments \"buffer_id\"))\n         (has-path (and (stringp path) (not (string-empty-p path))))\n         (has-buffer (and (stringp buffer-id)\n                          (not (string-empty-p buffer-id)))))\n    (when (eq has-path has-buffer)\n      (error \"provide exactly one of path or buffer_id\"))\n    (if has-buffer\n        (let* ((buffer (ende-project-buffer-for-id buffer-id root))\n               (buffer-path (ende-project-buffer-path buffer))\n               (relative (file-relative-name buffer-path root)))\n          (ende-tools--assert-path-authorized buffer-path root)\n          (list :kind 'buffer :buffer buffer :path buffer-path\n                :relative relative :buffer-id buffer-id))\n      (let* ((canonical\n              (ende-tools--assert-path-authorized\n               (ende-project-path path root) root))\n             (buffer (ende-project-visiting-buffer path root))\n             (relative (file-relative-name canonical root)))\n         (list :kind (if buffer 'buffer 'file)\n               :buffer buffer :path canonical :relative relative\n               :buffer-id (and buffer (ende-project-buffer-id buffer)))))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--resolve-disk-source (arguments root)\n  \"Resolve an authorized project file in ARGUMENTS below ROOT.\n\nThe returned source always reads from disk, even when a visiting buffer\nexists.  Visiting-buffer state is retained only as separate metadata.\"\n  (let* ((path-value (ende-tools--required-string arguments \"path\"))\n         (path (ende-tools--assert-path-authorized\n                (ende-project-path path-value root) root))\n         (relative (file-relative-name path root)))\n    (ende-project-assert-readable-file path)\n    (list :kind 'file :path path :relative relative\n          :visiting-buffer (ende-project-visiting-buffer relative root))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--source-kind-name (source)\n  \"Return the public source-kind name for SOURCE.\"\n  (if (eq (plist-get source :kind) 'buffer) \"buffer\" \"file\"))\n\n(defun ende-tools--source-visiting-buffer (source)\n  \"Return the buffer associated with SOURCE, if any.\"\n  (if (eq (plist-get source :kind) 'buffer)\n      (plist-get source :buffer)\n    (plist-get source :visiting-buffer)))\n\n(defun ende-tools--source-buffer-metadata (source)\n  \"Return visiting-buffer status for SOURCE without using its contents.\"\n  (let* ((buffer (ende-tools--source-visiting-buffer source))\n         (modified (and buffer\n                        (with-current-buffer buffer\n                          (buffer-modified-p)))))\n    (if (eq (plist-get source :kind) 'buffer)\n        (list :buffer_id (and buffer (ende-project-buffer-id buffer))\n              :buffer_modified modified)\n      (list :visiting_buffer (and buffer t)\n            :visiting_buffer_modified modified))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--with-disk-source (source function)\n  \"Call FUNCTION with detected coding system in a temporary disk buffer.\n\nSOURCE must be a disk source.  The temporary buffer does not visit the file or\nactivate a major mode.\"\n","truncated":true}}
