ende-chat-config--resource-names (names configuration)\n  \"Validate resource NAMES against CONFIGURATION.\"\n  (unless names\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"at least one resource is required\"))\n  (delete-dups\n   (mapcar (lambda (name)\n             (let ((name (ende-chat-config--name name \"resource\")))\n               (unless (assq name (plist-get configuration :resources))\n                 (ende-chat-config--error \"unknown resource: %s\" name))\n               name))\n           names)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--assign-resources (form configuration additive)\n  \"Apply resource assignment FORM to CONFIGURATION.\nWhen ADDITIVE is non-nil, union with each tool's current assignment.\"\n  (unless (ende-chat-config--proper-list-p form)\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"malformed resource assignment: %S\" form))\n  (let ((separator (cl-position :resources form)))\n    (unless (and separator (>= separator 2)\n                 (< separator (1- (length form))))\n      (ende-chat-config--error\n       \"resource assignment needs tools, :resources, and resources: %S\" form))\n    (let ((names (cl-subseq form 1 separator))\n          (raw-resources (nthcdr (1+ separator) form)))\n      (when (memq :resources raw-resources)\n        (ende-chat-config--error\n         \"resource assignment may contain only one :resources separator: %S\"\n         form))\n      (let ((tools (ende-chat-config--resolve-tools names configuration))\n            (resources (ende-chat-config--resource-names\n                        raw-resources configuration))\n            (assignments (plist-get configuration :grants)))\n        ;; Clear once so overlapping targets retain both origins.\n        (unless additive\n          (setq assignments\n                (cl-remove-if (lambda (entry) (memq (car entry) tools))\n                              assignments)))\n        (dolist (name (delete-dups names))\n          (dolist (tool (ende-chat-config--target-tools name configuration))\n            (let ((current (cdr (assq tool assignments)))\n                  (grants (mapcar (lambda (resource) (cons resource name))\n                                  resources)))\n              (setq assignments\n                    (ende-chat-config--put\n                     tool (delete-dups (append current grants)) assignments)))))\n        (plist-put configuration :grants assignments)))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--apply-form (form configuration)\n  \"Apply supported operation FORM to CONFIGURATION.\"\n  (unless (ende-chat-config--proper-list-p form)\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"configuration form must be a list: %S\" form))\n  (pcase (car form)\n    ('root\n     (unless (and (= (length form) 2) (stringp (cadr form)))\n       (ende-chat-config--error \"root expects one path string\"))\n     (plist-put configuration :root\n                (ende-chat-config--canonical-root (cadr form))))\n    ('group (ende-chat-config--define-group form configuration))\n    ('resource (ende-chat-config--define-resource form configuration))\n    ('tools\n     (unless (and (>= (length form) 2)\n                  (cl-every #'symbolp (cdr form)))\n       (ende-chat-config--error \"tools expects one or more tool or group names\"))\n     (dolist (name (cdr form))\n       (ende-chat-config--target-tools name configuration))\n     (plist-put configuration :active-targets\n                (delete-dups\n                 (append (plist-get configuration :active-targets)\n                         (cdr form)))))\n    ('clear-tools\n     (unless (= (length form) 1)\n       (ende-chat-config--error \"clear-tools takes no arguments\"))\n     (plist-put configuration :active-targets nil))\n    ('set-tool-resources\n     (ende-chat-config--assign-resources form configuration nil))\n    ('add-tool-resources\n     (ende-chat-config--assign-resources form configuration t))\n    ('clear-tool-resources\n     (unless (= (length form) 1)\n       (ende-chat-config--error \"clear-tool-resources takes no arguments\"))\n     (plist-put configuration :grants nil))\n    (_ (ende-chat-config--error \"unsupported form: %S\" (car form)))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--empty ()\n  \"Return fresh resolver state, including activation and grant provenance.\"\n  (list :root nil :groups nil :resources nil :active-targets nil\n        :grants nil :revoked-resources nil))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--normalize (configuration)\n  \"Convert resolver state CONFIGURATION to a runtime access policy.\"\n  (list :root (plist-get configuration :root)\n        :groups (plist-get configuration :groups)\n        :resources (plist-get configuration :resources)\n        :active-tools\n        (ende-chat-config--resolve-tools\n         (plist-get configuration :active-targets) configuration)\n        :tool-resources\n        (mapcar (lambda (entry)\n                  (cons (car entry) (delete-dups (mapcar #'car (cdr entry)))))\n                (plist-get configuration :grants))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--validate-access (normalized configuration)\n  \"Check active tools in NORMALIZED using CONFIGURATION for diagnostics.\"\n  (dolist (tool (plist-get normalized :active-tools))\n    (when (and (ende-tool-requires-resource (ende-tool--find tool))\n               (null (cdr (assq tool (plist-get normalized :tool-resources)))))\n      (let ((revoked (cdr (assq tool (plist-get configuration\n\t\t\t\t\t\t:revoked-resources)))))\n        (ende-chat-config--error\n         (concat \"active tool %s has no assigned resources%s; \"\n                 \"use (set-tool-resources %s :resources RESOURCE)\")\n         tool\n         (if revoked\n             (format \" after resource redefinition revoked %s\"\n                     (mapconcat #'symbol-name revoked \", \"))\n           \"\")\n         tool)))))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config--apply-blocks (blocks inherited)\n  \"Apply BLOCKS over INHERITED and return the resulting configuration.\"\n  (when (> (length blocks) 1)\n    (ende-chat-config--error \"only one config block is allowed per Org node\"))\n  (if (null blocks)\n      inherited\n    (let* ((configuration (ende-chat-config--copy inherited))\n           (ende-chat-config--block-line\n            (line-number-at-pos (org-element-property :begin (car blocks)) t))\n           (forms (ende-chat-config--read-forms\n                   (ende-chat-config--block-source (car blocks))))\n           (root-count 0)\n           group-names resource-names)\n      (unless (string-empty-p\n               (string-trim\n                (or (org-element-property :parameters (car blocks)) \"\")))\n        (ende-chat-config--error \"ende blocks do not accept parameters\"))\n      ;; Allow forward references.\n      (dolist (form forms)\n        (unless (ende-chat-config--proper-list-p form)\n          (ende-chat-config--error \"configuration form must be a list: %S\" form))\n        (pcase (car form)\n          ('group\n           (when (memq (nth 1 form) group-names)\n             (ende-chat-config--error \"group is defined twice in this block: %s\"\n                                      (nth 1 form)))\n           (setq configuration\n                 (ende-chat-config--define-group form configuration))\n           (push (nth 1 form) group-names))\n          ('resource\n           (when (memq (nth 1 form) resource-names)\n             (ende-chat-config--error \"resource is defined twice in this block: %s\"\n                                      (nth 1 form)))\n           (setq configuration\n                 (ende-chat-config--define-resource form configuration))\n           (push (nth 1 form) resource-names))\n          ('root\n           (cl-incf root-count)\n           (when (> root-count 1)\n             (ende-chat-config--error \"only one root form is allowed per block\"))\n           (setq configuration\n                 (ende-chat-config--apply-form form configuration)))))\n      (dolist (form forms)\n        (unless (memq (car-safe form) '(root group resource))\n          (setq configuration\n                (ende-chat-config--apply-form form configuration))))\n      configuration)))\n\n(defun ende-chat-config-resolve (tree parent-table headline)\n  \"Resolve chat tool configuration for HEADLINE in parsed Org TREE.\nPARENT-TABLE maps headlines to their parents.  Apply the document block,\nancestor blocks, and HEADLINE's own block, never preceding sibling blocks.\nReturn a normalized access policy.  Definitions need not be used.  A configured\nscope requires an explicit inherited or local root.  Invalid forms or active\ntools without required resources signal errors before dispatch.\"\n  (let* ((blocks-by-node (ende-chat-config--local-blocks tree))\n         (ancestry nil)\n         (current headline)\n         (configuration (ende-chat-config--empty))\n         (configured nil))\n    (while current\n      (push current ancestry)\n      (setq current (gethash current parent-table)))\n    (dolist (node (cons tree ancestry))\n      (let ((blocks (gethash node blocks-by-node)))\n        (when blocks\n          (setq configured t\n                configuration\n                (ende-chat-config--apply-blocks blocks configuration)))))\n    (when (and configured (null (plist-get configuration :root)))\n      (ende-chat-config--error \"an explicit root is required\"))\n    (let ((normalized (ende-chat-config--normalize configuration)))\n      (ende-chat-config--validate-access normalized configuration)\n      normalized)))\n\n(provide 'ende-chat-config)\n\n;;; ende-chat-config.el ends here\n","truncated":null}}

** Agent: Reply
:PROPERTIES:
:ENDE_ROLE: assistant
:ENDE_CHAT_REPLY_TO: question-20261004203011599234432-9
:ENDE_CHAT_REQUEST_ID: request-20261004203011599248422-10
:ENDE_MODEL: gpt-6-astra
:ENDE_REASONING_EFFORT: max
:ENDE_CHAT_CONTENT_HEADLINE_SHIFT: 2
:ENDE_CHAT_CONTENT_FORMAT: org
:END:

Yes—all three make sense. I would keep the existing resource/grant model, with two important boundaries: *URL permissions need their own matching rules*, and *patching must not bypass create/edit/delete permissions*.

*** Web fetch

**** Configuration and hostname matching

I would introduce explicitly typed resources, while keeping existing untyped resources as file resources for compatibility.

Proposed syntax—not supported by the current parser yet:

#+begin_src lisp
(resource web :type url
  (include "https://google.com/**")
  (include "https://*.google.com/**")
  (exclude "https://ads.google.com/**"))

(tools web_fetch)
(set-tool-resources web_fetch :resources web)
#+end_src

The current ~ende-chat-config.el~ treats resource patterns as file patterns. I would not stretch that matcher to cover URLs: parse and match the scheme, hostname, port, and path separately.

I’d define these semantics:

- ~google.com~ means exactly that hostname.
- ~www.google.com~ is a different hostname. *Do not automatically add or remove ~www~.*
- ~*.google.com~ matches subdomains, including deeper subdomains, but not ~google.com~ itself. It must not match ~evilgoogle.com~.
- ~/**~ means every URL path. It says nothing about subdomains.
- An omitted port means the scheme’s default port—not every port.
- HTTP and HTTPS require separate grants.

If someone only wants the apex and ~www~, explicitly listing both is better than granting every subdomain. Redirects between them must also satisfy the policy.

For convenience, I would support a bare hostname as shorthand:

#+begin_example
google.com    → https://google.com/**
,*.google.com  → https://*.google.com/**
#+end_example

That makes the common case pleasant without introducing ambiguous hostname/path globs such as ~google.com/**~.

Your ~bing.com~ exclusion is unnecessary unless some include would otherwise allow it: *no matching include means denied*.

**** Include/exclude composition

There is one subtlety in your existing file implementation: each resource means “included and not excluded,” and grants from multiple resources are then unioned.

Consequently, an exclusion in one resource does not override permission granted by another resource.

I would preserve that consistency for web resources and document it. If you want an absolute deny list, make that a separate policy layer rather than quietly giving ~exclude~ different meanings for files and URLs.

**** IP addresses and network safety

Support explicit IPv4 and IPv6 literals, using proper address parsing rather than string globs. IPv6 literals use brackets in URLs. I would leave CIDR rules out of the first version unless you already need them.

A hostname grant should not implicitly grant requests using that hostname’s IP address as the URL host.

More importantly, hostname matching is only half the authorization:

- Block loopback, private, link-local, and other non-public destinations by default—even when an allowed hostname resolves to them. Local services should require a separate, deliberate opt-in.
- Reauthorize every redirect before following it.
- Ensure the connection uses the vetted address; checking DNS and then letting the client independently resolve again leaves a DNS-rebinding gap.
- Keep TLS verification enabled. Do not inherit provider API headers, browser cookies, or ambient credentials. Proxy handling must not undermine destination checks.

For v1, I would keep fetching deliberately small: GET, bounded text/HTML responses, timeouts, a redirect limit, no JavaScript, and no automatic subresource fetching. Return the final URL, status, content type, and truncation information.

Also, fetching is not harmless merely because it does not modify local files: URLs and query parameters send information to the destination. Fetched content must remain untrusted data, not permission to invoke more tools.

*** Delete file

Yes—reuse the existing file resources and ~set-tool-resources~ mechanism unchanged. Deletion should have its own grant; permission to read or edit a file should not imply permission to delete it.

My suggested initial contract:

- Delete one existing regular file, not directories or recursive trees.
- Apply the same project-boundary and exclusion checks as other file tools.
- Reject final-component symlinks initially.
- Support an ~expected_digest~ precondition to detect stale targets.
- Refuse deletion when a visiting buffer has unsaved changes by default; never silently kill or discard that buffer.
- Respect a user-controlled trash/recovery policy, such as ~delete-by-moving-to-trash~.

The symlink point matters in this codebase: ~ende-project-path~ resolves existing paths through ~file-truename~. Simply resolving and then deleting could delete the symlink’s target rather than the link the caller named.

*** Multi-file patch

This is probably the most valuable addition for coding workflows. I would expose ~apply_patch~ and implement it as a *batch of validated file operations*, not as a loop over the existing immediately-mutating tool handlers.

A small, documented Codex-style add/update/delete grammar would be reasonable. Choose one format initially, reject unsupported constructs explicitly, and normalize it into your change-set machinery. Binary patches, symlink creation, and mode changes can wait.

**** Permissions

Give ~apply_patch~ its own file-resource scope, then also check the underlying operation’s grant:

- Updating requires edit permission.
- Adding requires create permission.
- Deleting requires delete permission.
- If moves are supported, require deletion permission on the source and creation permission on the destination.

This allows “patch these source files, but never delete them.” Otherwise, enabling patches could silently undo the distinction introduced by a separate delete tool.

Those grants need not expose every standalone tool to the model: tool activation and operation authorization can remain separate.

**** Batch behavior

I would use this sequence:

1. Parse the complete patch and enumerate every affected path.
2. Authorize every operation before reading or staging its contents.
3. Validate all hunks and preconditions, reject conflicting operations, and prepare all resulting contents.
4. Recheck sources and permissions before committing, with recoverable originals and explicit per-file results.

The important promise is:

#+begin_quote
A permission or validation failure changes no files.
#+end_quote

That is achievable and useful. “Fully atomic across multiple files” is a stronger promise that ordinary filesystem writes cannot generally provide, especially across crashes. If committing fails, attempt rollback and report exactly what changed and what was restored.

Keep the existing disk-versus-buffer distinction explicit. I would use the same conservative dirty-buffer policy as deletion rather than silently overwriting or reverting unsaved work.

*** Changes I would make underneath

Looking at the current implementation, three refactorings would support this cleanly:

- Extend resource requirements from the current yes/no flag to a resource kind: file, URL, or none.
- Track whether a tool needs a project root independently. Both configuration resolution and dispatch currently require a root; a web-only chat should not need one.
- Split mutation handling into reusable /prepare/ and /commit/ phases so single-file tools and batch patches share validation.

I would build the shared mutation layer and deletion first, then multi-file patches, then web fetching. The fetch API looks smallest, but its network authorization is the part that deserves the most careful testing.

** Tool implementation plan

#+begin_src text
Ok, implement it. Remember to produce elegant, idiomatic, readable, and efficient elip code. Test should be general and above all reasonable. I hate the usual narrow tests that you write. Commit as you go. Commit messages should be short and on-point---I have no time to read books of crap in there. On point, short, and human-readable. Don't fuck it up.
#+end_src
Make the plan in markdown, since that's the agent lingo

?= emacs\nEMACSCLIENT ?= emacsclient\n\n.DEFAULT_GOAL := check\n\n# All project Elisp files, excluding Git internals.\nEL_FILES := $(sort $(shell find . -type d -name .git -prune -o \\\n\t-type f -name '*.el' -print))\nEL_DIRS := $(sort $(dir $(EL_FILES)))\nTEST_FILES := $(filter %-test.el %-tests.el,$(EL_FILES))\n\nEMACS_BATCH = $(EMACS) -Q --batch \\\n\t$(foreach dir,$(EL_DIRS),-L \"$(dir)\") \\\n\t--eval '(setq load-prefer-newer t)'\n\n\n# These expressions must not contain shell single quotes.\ndefine CHECK_SYNTAX\n(let ((files command-line-args-left))\n  (setq command-line-args-left nil)\n  (dolist (file files)\n    (message \"Checking %s\" file)\n    (with-temp-buffer\n      (insert-file-contents file)\n      (emacs-lisp-mode)\n      (check-parens))))\nendef\n\n# Use absolute paths: the server may have a different working directory.\ndefine INDENT_FILES\n(let ((files (quote ($(foreach file,$(EL_FILES),\"$(abspath $(file))\")))))\n  (save-current-buffer\n    (dolist (file files)\n      (message \"Indenting %s\" file)\n      (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect file)\n        (save-excursion\n          (save-restriction\n            (widen)\n            (indent-region (point-min) (point-max))))\n        (save-buffer))))\n  (format \"Indented %d files\" (length files)))\nendef\n\n.PHONY: check lint syntax compile test format clean\n\n# Explicit sequencing also keeps \"make -j check\" safe.\ncheck:\n\t$(MAKE) clean\n\t$(MAKE) lint\n\t$(MAKE) test\n\nclean:\n\t$(RM) $(EL_FILES:.el=.elc)\n\ncompile:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) \\\n\t\t--eval '(setq byte-compile-error-on-warn t)' \\\n\t\t-f batch-byte-compile $(EL_FILES)\nformat:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) --eval '$(strip $(INDENT_FILES))'\n\nlint:\n\t$(MAKE) syntax\n\t$(MAKE) compile\n\nsyntax:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) --eval '$(strip $(CHECK_SYNTAX))' $(EL_FILES)\n\ntest:\n\t$(EMACS_BATCH) \\\n\t\t$(foreach file,$(TEST_FILES),-l $(file)) \\\n\t\t-f ert-run-tests-batch-and-exit\n","truncated":null}}

Provider requests\nfrom remote buffers are supported, but their processes always run locally.\"\n  (unless (and (stringp temporary-file-directory)\n               (file-name-absolute-p temporary-file-directory)\n               (not (file-remote-p temporary-file-directory))\n               (file-directory-p temporary-file-directory))\n    (user-error \"Ende requires an existing absolute local temporary-file-directory\"))\n  (file-name-as-directory (expand-file-name temporary-file-directory)))\n\n(defun ende-curl--loopback-host-p (host)\n  \"Return non-nil when HOST names an explicit loopback endpoint.\"\n  (and (stringp host)\n       (let ((name (downcase host)))\n         (or (string= name \"localhost\")\n             (string= name \"::1\")\n             (let ((parts (split-string name \"\\.\" nil)))\n               (and (= (length parts) 4)\n                    (cl-every\n                     (lambda (part)\n                       (and (string-match-p \"\\\\`[0-9]+\\\\'\" part)\n                            (<= (string-to-number part) 255)))\n                     parts)\n                    (= (string-to-number (car parts)) 127)))))))\n\n(defun ende-curl--validated-url (profile &optional url)\n  \"Return validated URL for PROFILE, optionally checking URL instead.\"\n  (let* ((value (or url (plist-get profile :url)))\n         (parsed (and (stringp value) (url-generic-parse-url value)))\n         (scheme (and parsed (downcase (or (url-type parsed) \"\"))))\n         (host (and parsed (url-host parsed)))\n         (auth-host (plist-get profile :auth-source-host))\n         (auth-user (plist-get profile :auth-source-user))\n         (auth-port (plist-get profile :auth-source-port)))\n    (unless (and (stringp value) (not (string-empty-p value))\n                 (member scheme '(\"http\" \"https\"))\n                 (stringp host) (not (string-empty-p host)))\n      (error \"Provider URL must be an absolute HTTP(S) URL\"))\n    (when (or (url-user parsed) (url-password parsed))\n      (error \"Provider URL must not contain user information\"))\n    (when (string-match-p \"[?#]\" value)\n      (error \"Provider URL must not contain a query or fragment\"))\n    (when (and (string= scheme \"http\")\n               (not (ende-curl--loopback-host-p host)))\n      (error \"Plain HTTP is only allowed for explicit loopback endpoints\"))\n    (when (and auth-host\n               (not (string= (downcase (format \"%s\" auth-host))\n                             (downcase host))))\n      (error \"auth-source host %s does not match endpoint host %s\"\n             auth-host host))\n    (when auth-host\n      (unless (and (stringp auth-user) (not (string-empty-p auth-user)))\n        (error \"Authenticated profiles require an explicit auth-source user\"))\n      (unless (and (integerp auth-port)\n                   (= auth-port\n                      (or (url-port parsed)\n                          (if (string= scheme \"https\") 443 80))))\n        (error \"Authenticated profile port does not match endpoint port\")))\n    value))\n\n(defun ende-curl--auth-secret (profile)\n  \"Resolve PROFILE's auth-source secret, or nil for unauthenticated hosts.\"\n  (ende-curl--validated-url profile)\n  (let ((host (plist-get profile :auth-source-host)))\n    (when host\n      (let* ((user (plist-get profile :auth-source-user))\n             (port (plist-get profile :auth-source-port))\n             (entries (auth-source-search :host host\n                                          :user user\n                                          :port port\n                                          :require '(:secret)\n                                          :max 1))\n             (entry (car entries)))\n        (unless entry\n          (error \"No auth-source entry for %s\" host))\n        (let ((secret (plist-get entry :secret)))\n          (setq secret (if (functionp secret) (funcall secret) secret))\n          (unless (and (stringp secret)\n                       (not (string-match-p \"[[:cntrl:]]\" secret)))\n            (error \"Invalid auth-source secret\"))\n          secret)))))\n\n(defun ende-curl--valid-header-p (header)\n  \"Return non-nil when HEADER is one complete RFC-style header line.\"\n  (and (stringp header)\n       (not (string-match-p \"[[:cntrl:]]\" header))\n       (string-match-p\n        \"\\\\`[!#$%&'*+.^_`|~0-9A-Za-z-]+:[ \\t]*.*\\\\'\" header)))\n\n(defun ende-curl--unsafe-profile-header-p (header)\n  \"Return non-nil when HEADER can affect routing, framing, or credentials.\"\n  (let* ((name (and (stringp header)\n                    (car (split-string header \":\"))))\n         (downcased (and name (downcase name))))\n    (and downcased\n         (or (member downcased\n                     '(\"host\" \"content-length\" \"transfer-encoding\"\n                       \"content-type\" \"accept\" \"connection\" \"trailer\"\n                       \"upgrade\" \"expect\" \"proxy-connection\"))\n             (string-match-p\n              \"\\\\(?:auth\\\\|token\\\\|key\\\\|secret\\\\|cookie\\\\|credential\\\\)\"\n              downcased)))))\n\n(defun ende-curl--config-escape (value)\n  \"Escape VALUE for a double-quoted curl config argument.\"\n  (mapconcat (lambda (character)\n               (if (memq character '(?\\\\ ?\\\"))\n                   (string ?\\\\ character)\n                 (char-to-string character)))\n             value \"\"))\n\n(defun ende-curl--config (profile &optional internal-headers)\n  \"Create a temporary curl config for PROFILE and INTERNAL-HEADERS.\nReturn nil when PROFILE and INTERNAL-HEADERS have no authentication or extra\nheaders.  INTERNAL-HEADERS are generated by a trusted transport and are kept\nseparate from user-configured profile headers.\"\n  (let* ((default-directory (ende-curl-local-directory))\n         (secret (ende-curl--auth-secret profile))\n         (headers (plist-get profile :request-headers))\n         (all-headers (append headers internal-headers\n                              (when secret\n                                (list (concat \"Authorization: Bearer \" secret))))))\n    (when (or secret headers internal-headers)\n      (dolist (header all-headers)\n        (unless (ende-curl--valid-header-p header)\n          (error \"Invalid request header\"))\n        (when (and (member header headers)\n                   (ende-curl--unsafe-profile-header-p header))\n          (error \"Unsafe request header must not be supplied by a profile\")))\n      (let ((contents\n             (mapconcat\n              (lambda (header)\n                (format \"header = \\\"%s\\\"\"\n                        (ende-curl--config-escape header)))\n              all-headers \"\\n\"))\n            (file nil)\n            (complete nil))\n        (unwind-protect\n            (progn\n              (setq file (make-temp-file \"ende-curl-\" nil \".conf\"))\n              (set-file-modes file #o600)\n              (with-temp-file file\n                (insert contents \"\\n\"))\n              (set-file-modes file #o600)\n              (setq complete t)\n              file)\n          (unless complete\n            (when (and file (file-exists-p file))\n              (condition-case nil\n                  (delete-file file)\n                ((error quit) nil)))))))))\n\n(defun ende-curl--network-options (url)\n  \"Return hardened common curl options for URL.\"\n  (let* ((parsed (url-generic-parse-url url))\n         (host (url-host parsed)))\n    (append\n     (list \"--globoff\" \"--proto\" \"=http,https\"\n           \"--proto-redir\" \"=http,https\")\n     (when (or (not ende-allow-environment-proxy)\n               (ende-curl--loopback-host-p host))\n       (list \"--noproxy\" \"*\")))))\n\n;;;; Shared curl command and resource APIs\n\n(defun ende-curl-base-command (url)\n  \"Return the common curl command prefix for URL.\"\n  (append (list \"curl\" \"--disable\"\n                \"--user-agent\" ende-client-name)\n          (ende-curl--network-options url)))\n\n(defun ende-curl-response-options (headers-file)\n  \"Return common curl options for a streamed response.\"\n  (list \"--silent\" \"--show-error\" \"--no-buffer\"\n        \"--dump-header\" headers-file))\n\n(defun ende-curl-stderr-filter (buffer process chunk)\n  \"Append CHUNK to BUFFER without exceeding the stderr resource ceiling.\"\n  (when (and (buffer-live-p buffer) (stringp chunk))\n    (let* ((used (or (and (processp process)\n                          (process-get process :ende-stderr-bytes))\n                     0))\n           (total (+ used (string-bytes chunk)))\n           (limit ende-max-stderr-bytes)\n           (remaining (and limit (- limit used)))\n           (prefix (if remaining\n                       (and (> remaining 0)\n                            (ende-transport-byte-prefix chunk remaining))\n                     chunk)))\n      (when (processp process)\n        (process-put process :ende-stderr-bytes total))\n      (when prefix\n        (with-current-buffer buffer\n          (goto-char (point-max))\n          (insert prefix))))))\n\n(defun ende-curl-timeout-options (&optional total-timeout max-filesize)\n  \"Return configured curl timeout and response-size options.\"\n  (append\n   (when ende-connect-timeout\n     (list \"--connect-timeout\"\n           (number-to-string ende-connect-timeout)))\n   (when total-timeout\n     (list \"--max-time\" (number-to-string total-timeout)))\n   (when max-filesize\n     (list \"--max-filesize\" (number-to-string max-filesize)))))\n\n(defun ende-curl-command (profile headers-file config-file)\n  \"Build curl command for PROFILE without embedding request data or secrets.\"\n  (ende-curl-json-command\n   profile headers-file config-file \"POST\" \"text/event-stream\" t))\n\n(defun ende-curl-json-command\n    (profile headers-file config-file method accept send-body &optional url)\n  \"Build a JSON curl command for PROFILE.\nMETHOD is the HTTP method, ACCEPT is the response media type, and SEND-BODY\ncontrols whether curl reads a request body from stdin.  Request data and\nsecrets are deliberately kept out of the command arguments.\"\n  (let ((url (ende-curl--validated-url profile url)))\n    (append\n     (ende-curl-base-command url)\n     (ende-curl-response-options headers-file)\n     (list \"--request\" method\n           \"--header\" \"Content-Type: application/json\"\n           \"--header\" (concat \"Accept: \" accept))\n     (when send-body (list \"--data-binary\" \"@-\"))\n     (when config-file (list \"--config\" config-file))\n     (ende-curl-timeout-options\n      ende-request-timeout ende-max-response-bytes)\n     (list \"--url\" url))))\n\n(defun ende-curl--cancel-idle-timer (process)\n  \"Cancel PROCESS's response inactivity timer, if any.\"\n  (let ((timer (process-get process :ende-idle-timer)))\n    (when timer\n      (cancel-timer timer)\n      (process-put process :ende-idle-timer nil))))\n\n","truncated":true}}

{"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende-core.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"6406e3deb7d20a21053105422054b53f91265f9974628898f797b5a380d998c0","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":267,"total_lines":267,"content":";;; ende-core.el --- Core definitions and utilities for ende -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter Samarin\n;; Author: Peter Samarin\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; Package-wide configuration, errors, and generic resource helpers.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'subr-x)\n\n(defgroup ende nil\n  \"Local-LLM text assistance: insert at cursor, improve, summarize.\"\n  :prefix \"ende-\"\n  :group 'convenience)\n\n(defvaralias 'ende-codex-log-tool-diagnostics 'ende-tool-diagnostics)\n\n(defcustom ende-tool-diagnostics t\n  \"Non-nil to retain tool diagnostics in job history for every provider.\n\nDiagnostics show the tool names sent and response item types.  They omit tool\narguments and response content.\"\n  :type 'boolean\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defconst ende-client-name \"ende-mode\"\n  \"Client name advertised in outbound HTTP requests.\")\n\n;;;; Transport and request resource options\n\n(defcustom ende-connect-timeout nil\n  \"Maximum seconds to wait while establishing an LLM connection.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"No connection deadline\" nil)\n                 (number :tag \"Maximum seconds\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-request-timeout nil\n  \"Maximum total seconds allowed for one LLM request.\n\nWhen nil, an active stream has no total wall-clock deadline.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"No total deadline\" nil)\n                 (number :tag \"Maximum seconds\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-response-idle-timeout 60\n  \"Maximum seconds without response-body data before a request fails.\n\nThe wait starts when the curl process starts and resets on each nonempty\nresponse chunk, including SSE heartbeat comments.  Headers alone do not\nreset it.  A server that sends nothing during prompt prefill can therefore\nreach this limit before generating output.  Increase it for slow prefill.\n\nThe value is captured when each request starts; changes affect new requests.\"\n  :type 'number\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-request-chars nil\n  \"Maximum combined characters in all messages sent for one request.\n\nWhen nil, do not impose a request-size limit.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum characters\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-source-name-disclosure t\n  \"Non-nil to include source file or buffer names in operation prompts.\n\nA provider profile can override this setting with its\n`:source-name-disclosure' value.  Source type and major mode are included\nregardless of this setting.\"\n  :type 'boolean\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-output-tokens nil\n  \"Maximum completion tokens requested from an LLM provider.\n\nWhen nil, omit the request limit and let the provider use its own maximum.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Provider default\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum tokens\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-response-bytes (* 256 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum number of response-body bytes accepted from one LLM request.\n\nThe default is deliberately generous so slow or verbose local inference is not\ntreated as an ordinary product limit.  Set it to nil to disable the breaker.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum bytes\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-sse-event-bytes (* 16 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum number of bytes accepted in one SSE line or event.\n\nThe default is deliberately generous.  Set it to nil to disable the breaker.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum bytes\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-output-chars 128000000\n  \"Maximum combined reasoning and completion characters retained per request.\n\nThe default is deliberately generous.  Set it to nil to disable the breaker.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum characters\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-response-header-bytes (* 1 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum response-header bytes parsed from curl's header output.\n\nThe default is a safety breaker rather than an ordinary response limit.  Set it\nto nil to disable the breaker.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum bytes\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-error-body-bytes (* 1 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum response bytes retained for provider error diagnostics.\n\nThe default limits only diagnostic previews, not successful response content.\nSet it to nil to retain complete error responses.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum bytes\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-oauth-refresh-bytes (* 1 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum response-body bytes accepted from an OAuth refresh request.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-max-stderr-bytes (* 16 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum stderr bytes retained from one curl process.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-provider-max-discovery-bytes (* 16 1024 1024)\n  \"Maximum response-body bytes accepted from provider model discovery.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-allow-environment-proxy nil\n  \"Non-nil to allow curl to honor ambient proxy environment variables.\nLoopback requests always bypass proxies.\"\n  :type 'boolean\n  :group 'ende)\n\n;;;; Shared face\n\n(defface ende-badge-face\n  '((t :foreground \"#facc15\" :weight bold))\n  \"Face used for ende badges.\"\n  :group 'ende)\n\n;;;; Errors and generic utilities\n\n(defun ende-trim-trailing (text)\n  \"Remove trailing newlines from TEXT, preserving inline trailing spaces.\"\n  (string-trim-right text \"[\\n\\r]+\"))\n\n(defun ende-validate-resource-options ()\n  \"Validate transport resource options before starting external processes.\"\n  (dolist (option (list (cons 'ende-connect-timeout ende-connect-timeout)\n                        (cons 'ende-request-timeout ende-request-timeout)))\n    (unless (or (null (cdr option))\n                (and (numberp (cdr option)) (> (cdr option) 0)))\n      (error \"%s must be nil or a positive number\" (car option))))\n  (unless (and (numberp ende-response-idle-timeout)\n               (> ende-response-idle-timeout 0))\n    (error \"ende-response-idle-timeout must be a positive number\"))\n  (dolist (option\n           (list (cons 'ende-max-output-tokens ende-max-output-tokens)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-response-bytes ende-max-response-bytes)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-sse-event-bytes ende-max-sse-event-bytes)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-output-chars ende-max-output-chars)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-response-header-bytes\n                       ende-max-response-header-bytes)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-error-body-bytes\n                       ende-max-error-body-bytes)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-oauth-refresh-bytes\n                       ende-max-oauth-refresh-bytes)\n                 (cons 'ende-max-stderr-bytes ende-max-stderr-bytes)\n                 (cons 'ende-provider-max-discovery-bytes\n ende-ignore-errors (function &rest arguments)\n  \"Call FUNCTION with ARGUMENTS while suppressing cleanup failures and quits.\n\nThis is the package-wide boundary for best-effort cleanup callbacks.\"\n  (condition-case nil\n      (apply function arguments)\n    ((error quit) nil)))\n\n(defun ende-bounded-single-line (value max-length &optional trim)\n  \"Return VALUE as a bounded, single-line string of MAX-LENGTH.\nWhen TRIM is non-nil, remove surrounding whitespace before bounding the\nresult.  The returned string uses an ellipsis when it is truncated.\"\n  (when value\n    (let ((text (replace-regexp-in-string\n                 \"[[:cntrl:]]+\" \" \" (format \"%s\" value))))\n      (when trim\n        (setq text (string-trim text)))\n      (unless (and trim (string-empty-p text))\n        (if (> (length text) max-length)\n            (concat (substring text 0 (- max-length 3)) \"...\")\n          text)))))\n\n(defun ende-owned-buffer (current name owner-variable initializer)\n  \"Return an owned buffer named NAME, initializing it with INITIALIZER.\nCURRENT is the package's existing buffer object, OWNER-VARIABLE is the\nbuffer-local ownership marker, and INITIALIZER is called in a new or reused\nowned buffer.\"\n  (if (buffer-live-p current)\n      current\n    (let* ((existing (get-buffer name))\n           (buffer (if (and existing\n                            (not (buffer-local-value owner-variable existing)))\n                       (generate-new-buffer name)\n                     (get-buffer-create name))))\n      (with-current-buffer buffer\n        (funcall initializer)\n        (set (make-local-variable owner-variable) t))\n      buffer)))\n\n(defun ende--safe-message-string (value)\n  \"Return VALUE as a bounded, single-line diagnostic string.\"\n  (ende-bounded-single-line value 1000))\n\n(defun ende-error-display-message (error)\n  \"Return a displayable message for structured or ordinary ERROR.\"\n  (cond\n   ((ende-error-p error)\n    (let ((kind (ende-error-kind error))\n          (message (ende--safe-message-string\n                    (or (ende-error-message error) \"unknown error\"))))\n      (if kind\n          (format \"%s: %s\" kind message)\n        message)))\n   ((stringp error) (ende--safe-message-string error))\n   ((and (consp error) (eq (car error) 'error))\n    (condition-case nil\n        (ende--safe-message-string (error-message-string error))\n      (error (ende--safe-message-string error))))\n   (t (ende--safe-message-string error))))\n\n(defun ende-plist-merge (base overrides)\n  \"Return BASE plist with OVERRIDES plist values applied.\"\n  (let ((result (copy-sequence base))\n        (rest overrides))\n    (while rest\n      (setq result (plist-put result (pop rest) (pop rest))))\n    result))\n\n(provide 'ende-core)\n\n;;; ende-core.el ends here\n","truncated":null}}

configuration :resources))))\n                (should (eq (ende-job-state (ende-ask)) 'completed))\n                (let* ((options (plist-get (car (last ende-test--calls)) :options))\n :tool-definitions))))\n                  (should (equal (sort names #'string<)\n                                 (sort (mapcar #'symbol-name (cadr case)) #'string<))))))))\n      (ende-test--reset)\n      (delete-directory root t))))\n\n\n\n(ert-deftest ende-test/chat-config-redefinitions-follow-the-org-tree ()\n  (let ((root (make-temp-file \"ende-config-scopes-\" t)))\n    (unwind-protect\n        (progn\n          (make-directory (expand-file-name \"src/private\" root) t)\n          (dolist (path '(\"main.el\" \"src/public.el\" \"src/private/secret.el\"))\n            (with-temp-file ende-chat-tools-test.el --- Chat tool tests -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; Coverage for tool dispatch loops and authorization integration.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require public (insert \"public text\\n\"))\n          (with-temp-file private (insert \"classified text\\n\"))\n          (let ((ende-llm-function\n                 (ende-test--fake-llm\n            \"(resource project (include \\\"**\\\") \"\n                 \"(exclude \\\"private/**\\\"))\\n\"\n                 \"(tools inspect)\\n\"\n                 \"(set-tool-resources inspect :resources project)\\n\"\n                 \"#+end_ende\\n\\n* Find classified text\\nSearch now\\n\")\n                root))\n              (org-mode)\n              (ende-test--chat-goto \"* Find classified text\")\n              (cl-letf (((symbol-function #'ende-tools--search-source)\n                         (lambda (source regexp before after maximum)\n                           (push (plist-get source :relative) visited)\n                           (funcall original-search source regexp before after\n                                    maximum))))\n                (ende-ask)\n                (let* ((document (ende-chat--build-document))\n                       (question (car (ende-chat--document-headlines document))))\n                  (setq frozen-configuration\n                        (ende-chat-config-resolve\n                         (ende-chat--document-tree document)\n                         (ende-chat--document-parent document) question))\n                  (goto-char (point-min))\n                  (should (search-forward\n                           \"(resource project (include \\\"**\\\")\" nil t))\n                  (replace-match\n                   \"(resource project (include \\\"private/**\\\")\" t t)\n                  (ende-test--complete-call (car ende-test--calls))\n                  (ende-test--complete-call (nth 1 ende-test--calls))))\n              (should (equal (nreverse visited) '(\"main.el\")))\n              (should (string-match-p \"Search complete\" (buffer-string)))\n              (should (= (length ende-test--calls) 2))\n              (let* ((first-call (car ende-test--calls))\n                     (definitions (plist-get (plist-get first-call :options)\n                                             :tool-definitions))\n                           (alist-get :function\n                                                           definition)))\n                                             definitions)\n                                     #'string<)\n                               '(\"list_project_files\" \"project_info\"\n                                 \"read_file\" \"search_project\")))\n                (should (equal (plist-get tool-reply :role) \"tool\"))\n                (should-not (string-match-p \"classified\"\n                                            (plist-get tool-reply :content)))\n                (let* ((reply (json-parse-string\n                               (plist-get tool-reply :content)\n                               :object-type 'hash-table))\n                       (result (gethash \"result\" reply)))\n                  (should (= (gethash \"files_inspected\" result) 1))))\n              (let* ((configuration frozen-configuration)\n                     (denied\n                      (ende-tool-call\n                       \"read_file\" '((\"path\" . \"private/secret.txt\"))\n                       root configuration))\n                     (allowed\n                      (ende-tool-call \"read_file\" '((\"path\" . \"main.el\"))\n                                      root configuration))\n                     (no-resources (copy-tree configuration)))\n                (should (plist-get allowed :ok))\n                (should (string-match-p\n                         \"public text\"\n                         (plist-get (plist-get allowed :result) :content)))\n                (should-not (plist-get denied :ok))\n                (should (string-match-p \"not authorized\"\n                                        (plist-get denied :error)))\n                (setf (plist-get no-resources :tool-resources) nil)\n                (should (string-match-p\n                         \"no assigned resources\"\n                         (plist-get\n                          (ende-tool-call\n                           \"read_file\" '((\"path\" . \"main.el\"))\n                           root no-resources)\n                          :error)))\n                (should (plist-get\n                         (ende-tool-call \"project_info\" nil root no-resources)\n                         :ok))))))\n      (ende-test--reset)\n      (when (file-directory-p root)\n        (delete-directory root t)))))\n\n\n\n(ert-deftest ende-test/chat-completions-tool-diagnostics-cover-local-and-openrouter ()\n  (should (eq ende-tool-diagnostics t))\n  (let* ((definition\n          (cl-find-if\n           (lambda (candidate)\n             (equal (alist-get :name (alist-get :function candidate))\n                    \"read_file\"))\n           (ende-tool-definitions)))\n         (tool-event\n          (ende-protocol-json-read-string\n           (concat\n            \"{\\\"choices\\\":[{\\\"delta\\\":{\\\"tool_calls\\\":[\"\n            \"{\\\"index\\\":0,\\\"id\\\":\\\"call-1\\\",\\\"type\\\":\\\"function\\\",\"\n            \"\\\"function\\\":{\\\"name\\\":\\\"read_file\\\",\"\n            \"\\\"arguments\\\":\\\"{\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"secret.el\\\\\\\"}\\\"}}]}}]}\"))))\n    (dolist (provider '(local openrouter))\n      (let* ((profile (list :name provider :model \"test-model\"\n                            :tool-definitions (list definition)))\n             (body (ende-llm--request-body\n                    (list (cons \"user\" \"read the file\")) profile))\n (lambda (event) (push event events)))\n               :tool-calls (make-hash-table :test #'eql))))\n        (ende-llm--diagnose-request session body)\n        (ende-llm--handle-json session tool-event)\n        (ende-llm--diagnose-result session)\n        (let ((diagnostics\n               (mapcar (lambda (event) (plist-get event :text))\n                       (cl-remove-if-not\n                        (lambda (event)\n                          (eq (plist-get event :type) :diagnostic))\n                        (nreverse events)))))\n          (should (equal diagnostics\n      \"secret\\\\.el\"\n                       (mapconcat #'identity diagnostics \"\\n\"))))))))\n\n;;;; Org-backed chat\n\n(ert-deftest ende-test/chat-tool-context-round-trips-to-provider-protocols ()\n  (let* ((transcript\n          (list (list :role \"assistant\" :content nil\n                      :tool-calls\n                      (list (list :id \"call-1\" :name \"read_file\"\n                                  :arguments \"{ \\\"path\\\" : \\\"foo.el\\\" }\")\n                            (list :id \"call-2\" :name \"read_file\"\n                                  :arguments \"{\\\"path\\\":\\\"bar.el\\\"}\")))\n                (list :role \"tool\" :tool-call-id \"call-1\"\n                      :content \"{\\\"text\\\":\\\"first line\\\\nsecond line\\\"}\")\n                (list :role \"tool\" :tool-call-id \"call-2\"\n                      :content \"{\\\"text\\\":\\\"another result\\\"}\")\n                (list :role \"assistant\" :content \"One more lookup.\"\n                      :tool-calls\n                      (list (list :id \"call-3\" :name \"read_file\"\n                                  :arguments \"{\\\"path\\\":\\\"third.el\\\"}\")))\n                (list :role \"tool\" :tool-call-id \"call-3\"\n                      :content \"{\\\"text\\\":\\\"third result\\\"}\")))\n         (encoded\n          (json-encode\n           (list\n            (cons :version 1)\n            (cons :messages\n                  (vconcat\n                   (mapcar\n                    (lambda (message)\n                      (pcase (plist-get message :role)\n                        (\"assistant\"\n                         (list (cons :role \"assistant\")\n                               (cons :content (plist-get message :content))\n                               (cons :tool_calls\n                                     (vconcat\n                                      (mapcar\n                                       (lambda (call)\n                                         (list\n                                          (cons :id (plist-get call :id))\n                                          (cons :name (plist-get call :name))\n :tool-call-id))\n                               (cons :content (plist-get message :content))))))\n                    transcript))))))\n         (decoded (ende-chat--decode-tool-context encoded))\n         (chat-assistant (ende-llm--message-json (car decoded)))\n         (chat-tool (ende-llm--message-json (nth 1 decoded)))\n         (responses (ende-codex--request-messages decoded)))\n    (should-not (string-match-p \"\\n\" encoded))\n    (should (equal decoded transcript))\n","truncated":true}}

Output:
{"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende-chat.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"a6152655114e5b5ccb8e97022c79521b7afc4d44162035b631d7259bcda4d44e","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":155,"total_lines":3349,"content":";;; ende-chat.el --- Org-backed chat for ende -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter\n;; Author: Peter\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools, llm\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; Chat treats an ordinary Org buffer as the durable conversation document.\n;; The only in-memory request state is the queue job and its destination marker;\n;; context is reconstructed from the current Org tree when a job starts.\n;;\n;; Durable headline properties include ENDE_ROLE (user or assistant) and\n;; ENDE_VISIBILITY (normal, hidden, or reasoning).  Generated response sections\n;; also retain ENDE_MODEL and, when used, ENDE_REASONING_EFFORT.  Roles inherit\n;; from the nearest marked ancestor; hidden visibility wins over every\n;; descendant.  Reasoning is normally sent only when\n;; `ende-chat-include-reasoning' is non-nil, except that a question asked from\n;; within a reasoning subtree includes its reasoning ancestors as context.\n;; Generated assistant/tool exchanges are stored in document order as sibling\n;; call and result sections inside an Agent: Work container. Older documents\n;; may still place calls below an intermediate reply.\n;; ENDE_CHAT_CONTEXT_FENCE marks a summary replacing earlier context.  The\n;; nearest fence on the question's ancestor path bounds the request; fences in\n;; other branches replace preceding siblings only within their own subtrees.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'json)\n(require 'org)\n(require 'org-element)\n(require 'org-fold)\n(require 'rx)\n(require 'subr-x)\n(require 'ende-core)\n(require 'ende-operation)\n(require 'ende-operations)\n(require 'ende-provider)\n(require 'ende-queue)\n(require 'ende-chat-config)\n(require 'ende-target)\n(require 'ende-ui)\n\n(declare-function ende-mode \"ende\" (&optional arg))\n\n;;;; Configuration and durable fields\n\n(defcustom ende-chat-buffer-name \"*ende-chat*\"\n  \"Name of the buffer used for chat when the source is not Org.\"\n  :type 'string\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-chat-context-policy 'root-prefix\n  \"Policy used when serializing a chat request.\n\n`root-prefix' includes eligible sections before the asked section in the\nactive level-one root, followed by the asked section's eligible body.\nContext fences substitute summaries for earlier history in their scopes.\n`current-section-only' includes only the current body.  Both policies also\ninclude reasoning ancestors when asked from within a reasoning subtree, and\nretain the current question as the final user message.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Root prefix\" root-prefix)\n                 (const :tag \"Current section only\" current-section-only))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-chat-include-reasoning nil\n  \"Non-nil to include reasoning-marked Org content in chat requests.\n\nHidden content remains excluded even when this is non-nil.  When a question is\nasked from within a reasoning subtree, its reasoning ancestors are included\nregardless of this setting.  Included reasoning keeps the effective user or\nassistant role of its enclosing section.\"\n  :type 'boolean\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-chat-compact-instruction\n  \"Summarize the conversation for continued work. Preserve the user's goals,\nrequirements and preferences, decisions and their rationale, important facts\nand identifiers, current progress, unresolved questions, and next steps.\nMerge earlier summaries with subsequent information, keeping corrections and\ndiscarding superseded details. Be concise but self-contained.\"\n  \"Default compaction instructions when the instruction section has no body.\"\n  :type 'string\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-chat-max-tool-rounds 100000\n  \"Maximum model responses that may request tools in one chat job.\"\n  :type 'integer\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defconst ende-chat--context-fence-title \"Agent: Context Fence\"\n  \"Host-owned title for a compaction result.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat--work-title \"Agent: Work\"\n  \"Host-owned container for intermediate agent protocol messages.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat-work-property \"ENDE_CHAT_WORK\"\n  \"Org property marking a generated, protocol-transparent work container.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat--tool-context-drawer \"ENDE_CHAT_TOOL_CONTEXT\"\n  \"Legacy Org drawer storing an assistant tool transcript for old chat files.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat--compact-instructions-title \"User: Context Fence Instructions\"\n  \"Title given to untitled, nonempty compaction instructions.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat-role-property \"ENDE_ROLE\"\n  \"Org property storing the durable chat role.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat-visibility-property \"ENDE_VISIBILITY\"\n  \"Org property storing the durable chat visibility.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat-id-property \"ENDE_CHAT_ID\"\n  \"Org property storing a durable identity for a chat question or reply.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat-reply-to-property \"ENDE_CHAT_REPLY_TO\"\n  \"Org property associating a reply with its question.\")\n\n(defconst ende-chat-request-id-property \"ENDE_CHAT_REQUEST_ID\"\n  \"Org property capabilities shared by queueing, target capture, and execution.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n\n(defcustom ende-insert-instruction\n  \"Write the most natural continuation of the text at the document, wherever they belong.\"\n  \"Instruction given to the LLM for `ende-write-anywhere'.\"\n  :type 'string\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-insert-anywhere-instruction\n  \"Insert the requested text in the document wherever it belongs.\"\n  \"Instruction given to the LLM for `ende-insert-anywhere'.\"\n  :type 'string\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(cl-defstruct (ende-operation-spec\n               (:constructor ende-operation-spec--create))\n  \"Static behavior for one ende operation.\"\n  type target-kind writes-buffer whole-buffer\n  allows-source-changes structured-result review-policy)\n\n(defconst ende-operation-specs\n  (list\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'insert :target-kind 'cursor-or-selection :writes-buffer t\n    :review-policy 'resolve)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'improve :target-kind 'selection-or-buffer :writes-buffer t\n    :review-policy 'resolve)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'summarize :target-kind 'selection-or-buffer\n    :allows-source-changes t\n    :review-policy 'ignore)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'rubber-duck :target-kind 'document :writes-buffer t\n    :whole-buffer t :review-policy 'resolve)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'chat :target-kind 'document :writes-buffer t\n    :allows-source-changes t :review-policy 'ignore)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'chat-compact :target-kind 'document :writes-buffer t\n    :whole-buffer t :allows-source-changes t :review-policy 'ignore)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'write-anywhere :target-kind 'selection-or-buffer :writes-buffer t\n    :whole-buffer t :allows-source-changes t :structured-result 'replacement\n    :review-policy 'resolve)\n   (ende-operation-spec--create\n    :type 'insert-anywhere :target-kind 'selection-or-buffer :writes-buffer t\n    :whole-buffer t :allows-source-changes t :structured-result 'insertion\n    :review-policy 'resolve))\n  \"Registered operation specifications.\")\n\n(defun ende-operation-for (type)\n  \"Return the specification for operation TYPE, or signal an error.\"\n  (or (cl-find type ende-operation-specs\n               :key #'ende-operation-spec-type :test #'eq)\n      (error \"Unknown ende operation: %s\" type)))\n\n(defun ende-operation-writes-buffer-p (type)\n  \"Return non-nil when TYPE can modify a buffer.\"\n  (let ((spec (cl-find type ende-operation-specs\n                       :key #'ende-operation-spec-type :test #'eq)))\n    (and spec (ende-operation-spec-writes-buffer spec))))\n\n(defun ende-operation-whole-buffer-p (type)\n  \"Return non-nil when TYPE examines the entire accessible document.\"\n  (let ((spec (cl-find type ende-operation-specs\n                       :key #'ende-operation-spec-type :test #'eq)))\n    (and spec (ende-operation-spec-whole-buffer spec))))\n\n(defun ende-operation-allows-source-changes-p (type)\n  \"Return non-nil when TYPE tolerates source changes during generation.\"\n  (let ((spec (cl-find type ende-operation-specs\n                       :key #'ende-operation-spec-type :test #'eq)))\n    (and spec (ende-operation-spec-allows-source-changes spec))))\n\n(defun ende-operation-structured-result (type)\n  \"Return TYPE's structured result kind, or nil for plain text results.\"\n  (let ((spec (cl-find type ende-operation-specs\n                       :key #'ende-operation-spec-type :test #'eq)))\n    (and spec (ende-operation-spec-structured-result spec))))\n\n(defun ende-operation-review-policy (type)\n  \"Return the pending-review policy for operation TYPE.\"\n  (let ((spec (cl-find type ende-operation-specs\n                       :key #'ende-operation-spec-type :test #'eq)))\n    (and spec (ende-operation-spec-review-policy spec))))\n\n(provide 'ende-operation)\n\n;;; ende-operation.el ends here\n","truncated":null}}

Output:
{"ok":true,"tool":"read_file","result":{"path":"ende-target.el","source_kind":"file","digest":"8d1674dfa5eae7280874ce89bbb46156fb9824e967bbe3007ab39d2f08482a5e","visiting_buffer":null,"visiting_buffer_modified":null,"start_line":1,"end_line":150,"total_lines":410,"content":";;; ende-target.el --- Buffer targets for ende jobs -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n\n;; Copyright (C) 2026 Peter\n;; Author: Peter\n;; Keywords: convenience, tools\n\n;;; Commentary:\n\n;; Stable buffer targets, source validation, restrictions, conflict checks,\n;; mutation monitoring, and queued insertion-anchor movement for ende.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'subr-x)\n(require 'ende-core)\n(require 'ende-review)\n\n(defcustom ende-target-max-context-chars nil\n  \"Maximum number of characters sent to the LLM as target context.\n  Tokens are estimated as chars/4.  When nil, send the complete target.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum characters\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(defcustom ende-target-window-chars nil\n  \"Characters kept on each side of the cursor when target context is elided.\"\n  :type '(choice (const :tag \"Unlimited\" nil)\n                 (integer :tag \"Maximum characters\"))\n  :group 'ende)\n\n(cl-defstruct (ende-target (:constructor ende-target--create))\n  \"A stable, restricted buffer target for a queued operation.\"\n  buffer start-marker end-marker cursor-marker placement\n  source-snapshot source-digest restriction-beg restriction-end selected offset\n  review-policy applying invalidated\n  kill-buffer-hook before-change-hook after-change-hook)\n\n;;;; Creation and positions\n\n(defun ende-target--make-marker (buffer position insertion-type)\n  \"Create a marker in BUFFER at POSITION with INSERTION-TYPE.\"\n  (let ((marker (make-marker)))\n    (set-marker marker position buffer)\n    (set-marker-insertion-type marker insertion-type)\n    marker))\n\n(defun ende-target--marker-position (marker)\n  \"Return MARKER's position, or nil when it has no live buffer.\"\n  (and (markerp marker) (marker-position marker)))\n\n(defun ende-target--anchor-position (anchor)\n  \"Return ANCHOR as a live marker position or numeric position.\"\n  (if (markerp anchor) (marker-position anchor) anchor))\n\n(defun ende-target-region-digest (start end)\n  \"Return a stable digest of the current buffer between START and END.\"\n  (secure-hash 'sha256 (current-buffer) start end))\n\n(cl-defun ende-target-create\n    (&key buffer start end cursor selected placement offset source-snapshot\n          (review-policy 'block))\n  \"Create a target in BUFFER from START to END anchored at CURSOR.\nSELECTED means START..END is immutable source.  PLACEMENT and OFFSET describe\nan insertion relative to that source.  SOURCE-SNAPSHOT is an optional textual\nfallback for callers that cannot capture a digest.  REVIEW-POLICY controls\nwhether pending reviews are checked for this target: `block' checks them,\n`resolve' leaves conflict resolution to the caller, and `ignore' permits\nread-only access to reviewed text.\"\n  (unless (buffer-live-p buffer)\n    (error \"target buffer no longer exists\"))\n  (setq review-policy (or review-policy 'block))\n  (unless (memq review-policy '(block resolve ignore))\n    (error \"unknown target review policy: %s\" review-policy))\n  (with-current-buffer buffer\n    (when (eq review-policy 'block)\n      (ende-review-assert-available buffer start end))\n    (ende-target--create\n     :buffer buffer\n     :start-marker (ende-target--make-marker buffer start (and selected t))\n     :end-marker (ende-target--make-marker buffer end nil)\n     :cursor-marker (ende-target--make-marker buffer cursor nil)\n     :placement placement\n     :source-snapshot source-snapshot\n     :source-digest (and selected\n                         (null source-snapshot)\n                         (ende-target-region-digest start end))\n     :restriction-beg (ende-target--make-marker buffer (point-min) nil)\n     :restriction-end (ende-target--make-marker buffer (point-max) t)\n     :selected selected\n     :offset offset\n     :review-policy review-policy)))\n\n(defun ende-target-start (target)\n  \"Return TARGET's current start position, or nil.\"\n  (ende-target--marker-position (ende-target-start-marker target)))\n\n(defun ende-target-end (target)\n  \"Return TARGET's current end position, or nil.\"\n  (ende-target--marker-position (ende-target-end-marker target)))\n\n(defun ende-target-cursor (target)\n  \"Return TARGET's current cursor position, or nil.\"\n  (ende-target--marker-position (ende-target-cursor-marker target)))\n\n(defun ende-target-live-p (target)\n  \"Return non-nil when TARGET's buffer is live.\"\n  (and (ende-target-p target)\n       (buffer-live-p (ende-target-buffer target))))\n\n(defconst ende-target--source-metadata-field-max-length 256\n  \"Maximum length of a source metadata field sent to an LLM.\")\n\n(defun ende-target--safe-buffer-name (name)\n  \"Return NAME without an absolute or remote path component.\"\n  (if (and (stringp name)\n           (or (file-name-absolute-p name)\n               (file-remote-p name)))\n      (file-name-nondirectory name)\n    name))\n\n(defun ende-target-source-metadata (target)\n  \"Return bounded source metadata for TARGET's buffer, or nil.\n\nVisiting files contribute only their basename.  Other buffers contribute their\nbuffer name unless it looks like an absolute or remote path.\"\n  (let ((buffer (and (ende-target-p target)\n                     (ende-target-buffer target))))\n    (when (buffer-live-p buffer)\n      (with-current-buffer buffer\n        (let* ((file-name (and (stringp buffer-file-name)\n                               (not (string-empty-p buffer-file-name))\n                               buffer-file-name))\n               (raw-name (if file-name\n                             (file-name-nondirectory file-name)\n                           (ende-target--safe-buffer-name\n                            (buffer-name))))\n               (mode (if (symbolp major-mode)\n                         (symbol-name major-mode)\n                       (format \"%s\" major-mode))))\n          (list :name (or (ende-bounded-single-line\n                           raw-name\n                           ende-target--source-metadata-field-max-length\n                           t)\n                          \"unnamed-buffer\")\n                :type (if file-name \"file\" \"buffer\")\n                :major-mode (or (ende-bounded-single-line\n                                 mode\n                                 ende-target--source-metadata-field-max-length\n                                 t)\n","truncated":true}}

(buffer-string)))\n\n(defun ende-chat--tool-argument-summary (arguments)\n  \"Return a compact display summary for JSON object ARGUMENTS, or nil.\"\n  (condition-case nil\n      (let ((object (ende-chat--response-property ende-chat-tool-kind-property kind)))\n\n(defun ende-chat--tool-call-text (level request-id call &optional result-properties)\n  \"Render normalized tool CALL at LEVEL (unless (and (stringp id) (not (string-empty-p id))\n                 (stringp name) (not (string-empty-p name))\n                 (stringp arguments))\n      (error \"Malformed provider tool call: %S\" call))\n    (concat (make-string level ?*) \" \"\n            (ende-chat--truncate-tool-heading (concat \"--> \" name) level) \"\\n\"\n            \":PROPERTIES:\\n\"\n \"\\n\\n\")))\n\n(defun ende-chat--assistant-protocol-text\n    (level question-id request-id result-properties normalized calls\n           &optional pending format)\n  \"Render one assistant protocol message and its ordered tool CALLS.\"\n  (let* ((format (if (eq format 'org) 'org 'literal))\n         (content (plist-get normalized :content))\n         (reply\n          (when (or (null calls)\n                    (and (stringp content) (not (string-blank-p content))))\n            (ende-chat--assistant-text\n             level question-id request-id\n             (concat result-properties\n                     (ende-chat--response-property\n                      ende-chat-content-format-property\n                      (symbol-name format))\n                     (when The temporary buffer does not visit the file or\nactivate a major mode.\"\n  (let ((path (plist-get source :path)))\n    (ende-project-assert-readable-file path)\n    (with-temp-buffer\n      (let ((coding-system-for-read 'undecided))\n        (insert-file-contents path))\n      (funcall function (or last-coding-system-used 'utf-8-unix)))))\n\n(defun ende-tools--with-source (source function)\n  \"Call FUNCTION with current source buffer selected for SOURCE.\n\nFor a disk source, a temporary buffer is used and the file is not visited.\nFUNCTION receives no arguments and may return any Refreshed Codex\n;; credentials must also be stored in a local file.\n\n;;; Code:\n\n(require 'cl-lib)\n(require 'ende-core)\n(require 'ende-operation)\n(require 'ende-provider)\n(require keymap for `ende-mode'.\"\n  (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))\n    (define-key map (kbd \"C-c e\") #'ende-selector-open)\n    map))\n\n(defvar ende-mode-map (ende--make-mode-map) \"Keymap for `ende-mode'.\")\n\n(defvar-local ende--mode-line-string \" ende[0]\"\n  \"Mode-line lighter showing jobs and pending reviews for the current buffer.\")\n\n(defun ende--job-targets-buffer-p (job
